## 9. Architecture and code review, 2026-09-12

The author's request for a comprehensive review adds a findings record to
this root before opening focused research children. The review covers the
engine, compiler, HTTP/CLI host, both client tiers and stores, React arms,
reference apps, simulator, fixtures, witnesses and release tooling at
Snapback 4 tree `cf0a13dca32b3e1721d1d947a3f82c262a0807f0`
(repository commit `d6c803878a5432cce5f045777786a049972d3d70`, version
0.0.16). It does not change the bakeoff decisions or accept new designs.

The prescribed suite passes, but additional probes reproduce
schema-admission and projection failures, lost or regressed replica state,
incorrect predictions, stale observations, compiler semantic errors and
request-worker starvation. Passing the existing suite does not establish
the missing properties. The author's updated scope reserves security holes
for another model; this record covers architecture, correctness,
performance and ergonomics and makes no security-review claim.

Four questions need design work beyond an isolated patch:

- [LLP 3000.000](./3000.000-atomic-schema-and-release-activation.research.md):
  the atomic unit and owner of schema/release activation.
- [LLP 3000.001](./3000.001-durable-replica-and-write-lifecycle.research.md):
  confirmed replica data, speculative writes and durable outcomes.
- [LLP 3000.002](./3000.002-end-to-end-resource-bounds.research.md):
  physical resource bounds across requests and replication.
- [LLP 3000.003](./3000.003-one-semantics-and-credible-verification.research.md):
  one semantic contract across Rust, TypeScript and verification hosts.

The [detailed review and 37-ticket index](./reviews/3000-architecture-code-review-20260912.md)
record evidence, alternatives and acceptance examples. All four children are
Draft Research. They remain review findings and proposals; the core owner
retains implementation custody under §1 decision 4.

The author subsequently requested implementation of the 37 concrete tickets.
All 37 are now closed; the [correction qualification record](./reviews/3000-review-corrections-20260913.md)
contains the executed checks, independent reviews and remaining research scope.
Their resolution records and the children's `Implementation follow-up`
sections describe the delivered boundaries: transactional semantic activation,
confirmed-base replay and atomic snapshot ownership, bounded admission/output,
and compiler/client parity corrections. Integration preserves the concurrently
landed native Expo module and ordered-merge performance work. The four children
remain Draft Research for their explicitly outstanding choices; the ticket
fixes do not claim that every architecture question has been settled.

The separate [security review](./reviews/3000-security-review-20260913.md)
opened twelve findings across the HTTP, identity, storage, synchronization and
asset boundaries. Its
[remediation record](./reviews/3000-security-remediation-20260913.md) closes
those findings and three overlapping comprehensive-review findings. The four
security decisions in §7 state the lasting contract; individual tickets retain
the counterexamples, acceptance evidence and limits.

## How to orient

Read this document, then LLP 2000.000 for the argument and its evidence,
then `snapback4/AGENTS.md` once it exists, then the journal. For the
sibling's position read LLP 2000; for the incumbent, LLP 1000; for the
history both inherit, LLP 0329 and its family.

## Non-claims

- This document accepts nothing in LLP 2000.000 on the author's behalf; it
  is the brief, and only running code binds.
- It changes no Snapback 2 or Snapback 3 status, scope, ceiling or gate, and
  edits neither rules file.
- It decides no promotion; the comparison and its ruling are the author's.
- It claims no result; every number above is a threshold or a plan.

## 2026-09-23 — Clique 0.3 follow-up implementation commission

Charlie commissioned Astra max or xhigh to implement the accepted
[modeling plan](./3000.011.000.001-modeling-after-clique-030.plan.md) and
[scale plan](./3000.011.001.001-scale-after-clique-030.plan.md). Their earlier
sanity review found no material concerns. Implementation preserves each plan’s
measurement-first sequence and stopping conditions. Historical research links
3000.000 and 3000.001 leave the current overlay to make room; the documents
remain. Package publication and mutation of the frozen benchmark are outside
this commission.

## 2026-09-27 — Global search beyond one read

Lane GS reproduced the global-search boundary and stopped at a design handoff.
The supervisor narrowed 0.4.6 to compact, bounded one-read membership search;
[LLP 3000.024](./3000.024-global-search-beyond-one-read.rfc.md) records the
Decision, implementation, measurements and explicit grouped-search refusal
beyond its envelope. The retained-session and stateless multi-pass contracts
remain future RFC work. Budgets, disclosure rules and the no-query-cache
exclusion are unchanged; round 1's report records qualification failures.

    49	    /// Record only implications that follow from count's nonnegative range.

    57	            (left.as_ref(), n, op.as_str())

    59	            let reversed = match op.as_str() { "<" => ">", "<=" => ">=", ">" => "<", ">=" => "<=", other => other };

    86	    pub(super) fn count_capture(&self, name: &str, value: &A) -> Option<(String, BTreeSet<String>)> {
    87	        let A::Call { name: call, args, .. } = value else { return None };

    96	    /// Attach pre-resolved capture-time provenance after assigning `name`.

    99	    pub(super) fn record_count_capture(&mut self, name: &str, capture: Option<(String, BTreeSet<String>)>) {
   100	        let Some((aggregate, mut owners)) = capture else { return };
   101	        let Some(result) = self.binding(name) else { return };
   102	        // The reaching definition is part of the marker. A later count over
   103	        // the same textual domain may restore a root marker after rebinding,
   104	        // but it must never make an older captured result current again.
   105	        let capture = vec![COUNT_CAPTURE.into(), aggregate,
   106	            format!("{}:{}", result.runtime, result.revision)];
   107	        owners.insert(name.to_owned());
   108	        for owner in owners {

   110	            binding.fields.insert(capture.clone(), Ty::Bool);

   115	    fn count_key(&self, value: &A) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
   116	        let (count_arg, captured_name) = match value {
   117	            A::Call { name, args, .. } if name == "count" && args.len() == 1
   118	                && !self.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) => args.first(),
   119	            A::Name { name, .. } => self.binding(name)?.origins.iter().rev().find_map(|(_, origin)| match origin {
   120	                A::Call { name, args, .. } if name == "count" && args.len() == 1
   121	                    && !self.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) => args.first(),
   122	                _ => None,
   123	            }),
   124	            _ => None,
   125	        }.map(|arg| (arg, match value { A::Name { name, .. } => Some(name.as_str()), _ => None }))?;
   126	        let result = self.aggregate_key(&self.aggregate_domain(count_arg)?)?;
   127	        if let Some(name) = captured_name {
   128	            let binding = self.binding(name)?;
   129	            let definition = format!("{}:{}", binding.runtime, binding.revision);
   130	            let capture = binding.fields.keys().find(|path| path.len() == 3
   131	                && path[0] == COUNT_CAPTURE && path[1] == result.0[1]
   132	                && path[2] == definition)?.clone();
   133	            if binding.fields.get(&capture) != Some(&Ty::Bool)
   134	                || !result.1.iter().all(|root| self.binding(root)
   135	                    .is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.get(&capture) == Some(&Ty::Bool)))
   136	            {
   137	                return None;
   138	            }
   139	        }
   140	        Some(result)
   141	    }
   142	
   143	    fn aggregate_key(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
   144	        if read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() { return None; }
   145	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
   146	        let keys = read.keys.iter().map(|key| Some(format!("{:?}:{}", key.column,
   147	            self.aggregate_value(&key.value, &mut roots, false)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
   148	        let seeks = read.seeks.iter().map(|seek| Some(format!("{}:{}:{}", seek.column, seek.op,
   149	            self.aggregate_value(&seek.value, &mut roots, false)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
   150	        let filter = match read.filter.as_deref() {
   151	            Some(value) => Some(self.aggregate_value(value, &mut roots, true)?),
   152	            None => None,
   153	        };
   154	        if roots.is_empty() { roots.insert(pure_calls::CONSTANTS.into()); }
   155	        let identity = format!("{:?}:{:?}:{:?}:{keys:?}:{seeks:?}:{filter:?}", read.table, read.index, read.order_keys);
   156	        Some((vec![NONEMPTY.into(), identity], roots))
   157	    }
   158	
   159	    fn aggregate_value(&self, value: &A, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>, domain_focal: bool) -> Option<String> {
   160	        match value {
   161	            // A dot in the read's filter is its candidate source row. Dots in
   162	            // keys/seeks are the enclosing lexical shape row and must retain
   163	            // that row variable's unique snapshot identity.
   164	            A::Focal { .. } if domain_focal => Some("domain-focal".into()),
   165	            A::Member { base, name, .. } if domain_focal && matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. }) => Some(format!("domain-focal.{name}")),
   166	            // The operation clock is one immutable input. `new` is excluded:
   167	            // evaluating the same source again would mint a different id.
   168	            A::Name { name, .. } if matches!(name.as_str(), "now" | "commit") => Some(format!("clock:{name}")),
   169	            A::Literal { .. } | A::Name { .. } | A::Member { .. } | A::Focal { .. } => self.stable_value(value, roots).map(|value| value.key()),
   170	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } => Some(format!("unary:{op}:{}", self.aggregate_value(value, roots, domain_focal)?)),
   171	            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => Some(format!("binary:{op}:{}:{}", self.aggregate_value(left, roots, domain_focal)?, self.aggregate_value(right, roots, domain_focal)?)),
   172	            A::Call { name, args, .. } => {
   173	                if let Some(function) = self.catalog.functions.get(name) {
   174	                    if self.pure_functions.get(&function.name) != Some(&true) { return None; }
   175	                    return Some(format!("call:{name}:{:?}", args.iter().map(|arg| self.aggregate_value(arg, roots, domain_focal)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?));
   176	                }
   177	                if !stable_builtin(name, args.len()) { return None; }
   178	                Some(format!("builtin:{name}:{:?}", args.iter().map(|arg| self.aggregate_value(arg, roots, domain_focal)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?))
   179	            }
   180	            A::Array { items, .. } => Some(format!("array:{:?}", items.iter().map(|item| self.aggregate_value(item, roots, domain_focal)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?)),
   181	            _ => None,
   182	        }
   183	    }
   184	}
   185	
   186	/// Source-level builtins whose result is a deterministic function of stable
   187	/// arguments. Arguments containing reads or allocation do not canonicalize;
   188	/// malformed calls still receive their ordinary binding error.
   189	fn stable_builtin(name: &str, arity: usize) -> bool {
   190	    match name {
   191	        "count" | "first" | "last" | "lower" | "trim" | "sha256" | "tokens"
   192	        | "month" | "year" | "monthStart" | "yearStart" => arity == 1,
   193	        "at" | "group" | "join" | "min" | "max" => arity == 2,
   194	        "slice" | "share" => arity == 3,
   195	        "sum" => matches!(arity, 1 | 2),
   196	        "day" | "hour" => matches!(arity, 1 | 2),
   197	        "bucket" => matches!(arity, 2 | 3),
   198	        "divide" => matches!(arity, 3 | 4),
   199	        "sort" => arity >= 2,
   200	        _ => false,
   201	    }
   202	}

                 .strip_prefix("https://")

+            // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#1-decision — one deterministic ASCII mailbox grammar is shared by arguments, writes and devices.
+            Self::Email => {
+                if text.len() > 254 { return false; }
+                let Some((local, domain)) = text.split_once('@') else { return false };
+                if domain.contains('@') || local.is_empty() || local.len() > 64
+                    || domain.is_empty() || domain.len() > 253
+                    || local.starts_with('.') || local.ends_with('.') || local.contains("..")
+                {
+                    return false;
+                }
+                let local_ok = local.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric()
+                    || matches!(b, b'.' | b'!' | b'#' | b'$' | b'%' | b'&' | b'\'' | b'*'
+                        | b'+' | b'/' | b'=' | b'?' | b'^' | b'_' | b'`' | b'{' | b'|'
+                        | b'}' | b'~' | b'-'));
+                local_ok && domain.split('.').all(|label| !label.is_empty() && label.len() <= 63
+                    && !label.starts_with('-') && !label.ends_with('-')
+                    && label.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-'))
+            }
         }
     }
 }

     1	//! Exact non-empty facts established by successful count guards.
     2	
     3	use super::*;
     4	use std::collections::BTreeSet;

     6	pub(super) const NONEMPTY: &str = "$aggregateNonempty";
     7	/// Capture marker for a local whose current value is an exact `count(domain)`
     8	/// observation. It lives on the result and every domain root: ordinary
     9	/// rebinding, a write, an authority edge, or a control-flow meet therefore
    10	/// destroys stale provenance without repurposing diagnostic `origins`.
    11	pub(super) const COUNT_CAPTURE: &str = "$aggregateCountCapture";
    12	
    13	impl Binder<'_> {
    14	    /// A non-empty observation survives evaluation of its guard only when the
    15	    /// complete condition is write-free. Unlike point facts, aggregate facts
    16	    /// deliberately admit filters and seeks because those are part of the
    17	    /// exact domain identity recorded below.
    18	    pub(super) fn aggregate_guard_safe(&self, value: &A) -> bool {
    19	        match value {
    20	            A::Name { .. } | A::Focal { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => true,
    21	            A::Array { items, .. } => items.iter().all(|item| self.aggregate_guard_safe(item)),
    22	            A::Object { fields, .. } => fields.iter().all(|field| field.value.as_ref().is_none_or(|value| self.aggregate_guard_safe(value))),
    23	            A::Unary { value, .. } | A::Assert { value, .. } | A::ShapeUse { base: value, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(value),
    24	            A::Binary { left, right, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(left) && self.aggregate_guard_safe(right),
    25	            A::Member { base, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(base),
    26	            A::Call { name, args, .. } => self.catalog.functions.get(name).is_none_or(|function|
    27	                self.pure_functions.get(&function.name) == Some(&true))
    28	                && args.iter().all(|arg| self.aggregate_guard_safe(arg)),
    29	            A::Read(read) | A::Exists { read, .. } | A::Created { read, .. } => {
    30	                read.computed.values().all(|value| self.aggregate_guard_safe(value))
    31	                    && read.keys.iter().all(|key| self.aggregate_guard_safe(&key.value))
    32	                    && read.seeks.iter().all(|seek| self.aggregate_guard_safe(&seek.value))
    33	                    && read.filter.as_deref().is_none_or(|filter| self.aggregate_guard_safe(filter))
    34	                    && read.matching.as_deref().is_none_or(|matching| self.aggregate_guard_safe(matching))
    35	                    && read.range.as_ref().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_deref()).is_none_or(|cursor| self.aggregate_guard_safe(cursor))
    36	            }
    37	            A::Shape { base, fields, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(base)
    38	                && fields.iter().all(|field| match field {
    39	                    ShapeField::Field { value, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(value),
    40	                    ShapeField::Spread { .. } | ShapeField::NamedSpread { .. } | ShapeField::Bare { .. } => true,
    41	                }),
    42	            A::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(then)
    43	                && self.aggregate_guard_safe(condition)
    44	                && self.aggregate_guard_safe(otherwise),
    45	            A::Merge(_) | A::Walk { .. } | A::Write { .. } => false,
    46	        }
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    /// Record only implications that follow from count's nonnegative range.
    50	    /// The common authored form is `require n > 0`, where `n` was bound from
    51	    /// `count(domain)`; direct count calls use the same identity.
    52	    // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#4-non-empty-domain-proof — the fact belongs to the exact domain roots and transaction state.
    53	    pub(super) fn record_aggregate_nonempty(&mut self, condition: &A, truth: bool) {
    54	        if !truth { return; }
    55	        let A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = condition else { return };
    56	        let (candidate, threshold, op) = if let Some(n) = literal_int(right) {
    57	            (left.as_ref(), n, op.as_str())
    58	        } else if let Some(n) = literal_int(left) {
    59	            let reversed = match op.as_str() { "<" => ">", "<=" => ">=", ">" => "<", ">=" => "<=", other => other };
    60	            (right.as_ref(), n, reversed)
    61	        } else { return };
    62	        let proves_positive = match op {
    63	            ">" => threshold >= 0,
    64	            ">=" | "=" => threshold >= 1,
    65	            "!=" => threshold == 0,
    66	            _ => false,
    67	        };
    68	        if !proves_positive { return; }
    69	        let Some((key, roots)) = self.count_key(candidate) else { return };
    70	        for root in roots {
    71	            let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&root).cloned() else { return };
    72	            binding.fields.insert(key.clone(), Ty::Bool);
    73	            self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(root, binding);
    74	        }
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    pub(super) fn aggregate_nonempty(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
    78	        self.aggregate_key(read).is_some_and(|(key, roots)| roots.iter().all(|root|
    79	            self.binding(root).is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.get(&key) == Some(&Ty::Bool))))
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    /// Resolve count provenance before rebinding `name`: a count domain may
    83	    /// itself refer to the old value of that local. Such a self-dependent
    84	    /// capture cannot be represented by the post-assignment binding and is
    85	    /// therefore deliberately not narrowed.
    86	    pub(super) fn count_capture(&self, name: &str, value: &A) -> Option<(String, BTreeSet<String>)> {
    87	        let A::Call { name: call, args, .. } = value else { return None };
    88	        if call != "count" || args.len() != 1 || self.catalog.functions.contains_key(call)
    89	            || !self.aggregate_guard_safe(value) { return None; }
    90	        let read = args.first().and_then(|arg| self.aggregate_domain(arg))?;
    91	        let (aggregate, roots) = self.aggregate_key(&read)?;
    92	        if roots.contains(name) { return None; }
    93	        Some((aggregate[1].clone(), roots))
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    /// Attach pre-resolved capture-time provenance after assigning `name`.
    97	    /// `origins` remains diagnostic history; this field fact is the reaching
    98	    /// definition proof used by a later `require name > 0`.
    99	    pub(super) fn record_count_capture(&mut self, name: &str, capture: Option<(String, BTreeSet<String>)>) {
   100	        let Some((aggregate, mut owners)) = capture else { return };
   101	        let Some(result) = self.binding(name) else { return };
   102	        // The reaching definition is part of the marker. A later count over
   103	        // the same textual domain may restore a root marker after rebinding,
   104	        // but it must never make an older captured result current again.
   105	        let capture = vec![COUNT_CAPTURE.into(), aggregate,
   106	            format!("{}:{}", result.runtime, result.revision)];
   107	        owners.insert(name.to_owned());
   108	        for owner in owners {
   109	            let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&owner).cloned() else { return };
   110	            binding.fields.insert(capture.clone(), Ty::Bool);
   111	            self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(owner, binding);
   112	        }
   113	    }
   114	
   115	    fn count_key(&self, value: &A) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
   116	        let (count_arg, captured_name) = match value {
   117	            A::Call { name, args, .. } if name == "count" && args.len() == 1
   118	                && !self.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) => args.first(),
   119	            A::Name { name, .. } => self.binding(name)?.origins.iter().rev().find_map(|(_, origin)| match origin {
   120	                A::Call { name, args, .. } if name == "count" && args.len() == 1
   121	                    && !self.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) => args.first(),
   122	                _ => None,
   123	            }),
   124	            _ => None,
   125	        }.map(|arg| (arg, match value { A::Name { name, .. } => Some(name.as_str()), _ => None }))?;
   126	        let result = self.aggregate_key(&self.aggregate_domain(count_arg)?)?;
   127	        if let Some(name) = captured_name {
   128	            let binding = self.binding(name)?;
   129	            let definition = format!("{}:{}", binding.runtime, binding.revision);
   130	            let capture = binding.fields.keys().find(|path| path.len() == 3
   131	                && path[0] == COUNT_CAPTURE && path[1] == result.0[1]
   132	                && path[2] == definition)?.clone();
   133	            if binding.fields.get(&capture) != Some(&Ty::Bool)
   134	                || !result.1.iter().all(|root| self.binding(root)
   135	                    .is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.get(&capture) == Some(&Ty::Bool)))
   136	            {
   137	                return None;
   138	            }
   139	        }
   140	        Some(result)
   141	    }
   142	
   143	    fn aggregate_key(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
   144	        if read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() { return None; }
   145	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
   146	        let keys = read.keys.iter().map(|key| Some(format!("{:?}:{}", key.column,
   147	            self.aggregate_value(&key.value, &mut roots, false)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
   148	        let seeks = read.seeks.iter().map(|seek| Some(format!("{}:{}:{}", seek.column, seek.op,
   149	            self.aggregate_value(&seek.value, &mut roots, false)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
   150	        let filter = match read.filter.as_deref() {
   151	            Some(value) => Some(self.aggregate_value(value, &mut roots, true)?),
   152	            None => None,
   153	        };
   154	        if roots.is_empty() { roots.insert(pure_calls::CONSTANTS.into()); }
   155	        let identity = format!("{:?}:{:?}:{:?}:{keys:?}:{seeks:?}:{filter:?}", read.table, read.index, read.order_keys);
   156	        Some((vec![NONEMPTY.into(), identity], roots))
   157	    }
   158	
   159	    fn aggregate_value(&self, value: &A, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>, domain_focal: bool) -> Option<String> {
   160	        match value {
   161	            // A dot in the read's filter is its candidate source row. Dots in
   162	            // keys/seeks are the enclosing lexical shape row and must retain
   163	            // that row variable's unique snapshot identity.
   164	            A::Focal { .. } if domain_focal => Some("domain-focal".into()),
   165	            A::Member { base, name, .. } if domain_focal && matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. }) => Some(format!("domain-focal.{name}")),
   166	            // The operation clock is one immutable input. `new` is excluded:
   167	            // evaluating the same source again would mint a different id.
   168	            A::Name { name, .. } if matches!(name.as_str(), "now" | "commit") => Some(format!("clock:{name}")),
   169	            A::Literal { .. } | A::Name { .. } | A::Member { .. } | A::Focal { .. } => self.stable_value(value, roots).map(|value| value.key()),
   170	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } => Some(format!("unary:{op}:{}", self.aggregate_value(value, roots, domain_focal)?)),
   171	            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => Some(format!("binary:{op}:{}:{}", self.aggregate_value(left, roots, domain_focal)?, self.aggregate_value(right, roots, domain_focal)?)),
   172	            A::Call { name, args, .. } => {
   173	                if let Some(function) = self.catalog.functions.get(name) {
   174	                    if self.pure_functions.get(&function.name) != Some(&true) { return None; }
   175	                    return Some(format!("call:{name}:{:?}", args.iter().map(|arg| self.aggregate_value(arg, roots, domain_focal)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?));
   176	                }
   177	                if !stable_builtin(name, args.len()) { return None; }
   178	                Some(format!("builtin:{name}:{:?}", args.iter().map(|arg| self.aggregate_value(arg, roots, domain_focal)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?))
   179	            }
   180	            A::Array { items, .. } => Some(format!("array:{:?}", items.iter().map(|item| self.aggregate_value(item, roots, domain_focal)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?)),
   181	            _ => None,
   182	        }
   183	    }
   184	}
   185	
   186	/// Source-level builtins whose result is a deterministic function of stable
   187	/// arguments. Arguments containing reads or allocation do not canonicalize;
   188	/// malformed calls still receive their ordinary binding error.
   189	fn stable_builtin(name: &str, arity: usize) -> bool {
   190	    match name {
   191	        "count" | "first" | "last" | "lower" | "trim" | "sha256" | "tokens"
   192	        | "month" | "year" | "monthStart" | "yearStart" => arity == 1,
   193	        "at" | "group" | "join" | "min" | "max" => arity == 2,
   194	        "slice" | "share" => arity == 3,
   195	        "sum" => matches!(arity, 1 | 2),
   196	        "day" | "hour" => matches!(arity, 1 | 2),
   197	        "bucket" => matches!(arity, 2 | 3),
   198	        "divide" => matches!(arity, 3 | 4),
   199	        "sort" => arity >= 2,
   200	        _ => false,
   201	    }
   202	}

   162	    /// Binding state is keyed by runtime identity so a same-named loop item

   164	    breaks: Vec<BTreeMap<String, Binding>>,
   165	    continues: Vec<BTreeMap<String, Binding>>,
   166	}
   167	
   168	const MAX_EXPANSION_DEPTH: u32 = 8;
   169	const MENTION_MAX: u32 = 64;

   171	impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
   172	    pub fn bind(catalog: &'a Catalog, schema: &'a snapback4_core::schema::Schema, module: &str, decl: &ast::ProgramDecl, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Option<BoundProgram> {

   174	    }
   175	
   176	    /// Differential-test control: lowering is identical with the side map disabled.
   177	    #[doc(hidden)]
   178	    pub fn bind_with_ownership(catalog: &'a Catalog, schema: &'a snapback4_core::schema::Schema, module: &str, decl: &ast::ProgramDecl, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>, record_ownership: bool) -> Option<BoundProgram> {
   179	        let mut binder = Binder { viewer_depth: None, maintenance_scopes: BTreeMap::new(), pure_functions: BTreeMap::new(), call_types: BTreeMap::new(), page_sources: BTreeMap::new(), canonical_sources: BTreeMap::new(), constraint_domains: Default::default(), filters: BTreeMap::new(), variants: Vec::new(), view_types: BTreeMap::new(), sources: BTreeMap::new(), selections: BTreeMap::new(), presence_sources: Vec::new(), receipt_sources: Vec::new(), receipt_returns: Vec::new(), implicit_write_results: Vec::new(), catalog, schema, name: decl.name.clone(), kind: decl.kind, caller_kind: decl.kind, call_chain: Vec::new(), job_bound_helpers: Default::default(), scopes: vec![BTreeMap::new()], focal: None, in_predicate: false, returned_data: false, shape_field: None, temps: 0, sites: 0, returns: Vec::new(), depth: 0, loops: 0, loop_transfers: Vec::new(), caller_loops: 0, in_prelude: false, total: None, record_ownership, expanding_feed: None, timeline_scans: Default::default(), notes: Vec::new(), errors: Vec::new(), pending_totals: Vec::new() };
   180	        binder.call_chain.push((super::operation_name(module, &decl.name), decl.site.clone()));

   186	                diagnostics.push(unknown(&arg.site, format!("duplicate argument {}", arg.name), "rename one argument"));
   187	                return None;
   188	            }
   189	            if let ast::TypeKind::Ref { table } = &arg.type_.kind
   190	                && !catalog.tables.contains_key(table)
   191	            {
   192	                diagnostics.push(type_error(&arg.site, format!("argument {} references unknown table {table}", arg.name), name_rewrite(table, catalog.tables.keys().map(String::as_str), None)));
   193	                return None;
   194	            }
   195	            let ty = Ty::from_scalar(&arg.type_);
   196	            binder.scopes[0].insert(arg.name.clone(), Binding { runtime: arg.name.clone(), ty: ty.clone(), declared: ty.clone(), parameter: true, revision: 0, fields: BTreeMap::new(), error: None, origins: Vec::new() });

   198	            arg_types.push((arg.name.clone(), ty));
   199	        }

   202	            // module is checked against the declaration by `check`.
   203	            let full_name = super::operation_name(module, &decl.name);

   205	                ProgramKind::Query => (ir::ProgramKind::Query, Ty::Json),
   206	                _ => (ir::ProgramKind::Job, Ty::Null),
   207	            };
   208	            return Some(BoundProgram { maintenance_scopes: binder.maintenance_scopes, statement_ends: Vec::new(), page_sources: binder.page_sources, canonical_sources: binder.canonical_sources, constraint_domains: binder.constraint_domains, filters: binder.filters, view_types: binder.view_types, selections: binder.selections, sources: binder.sources, receipt_sources: binder.receipt_sources, receipt_returns: binder.receipt_returns, implicit_write_results: binder.implicit_write_results, timeline_scans: binder.timeline_scans, program: Program { name: full_name, kind, args, read_time_granularity_ms: None, body: Vec::new(), native: true, returned_shapes: Default::default() }, presence: None, module: module.to_owned(), args: arg_types, result, notes: binder.notes, site: decl.site.clone(), job_bound_helpers: binder.job_bound_helpers });
   209	        }
   210	        let mut body = setup::Body::default();
   211	        let mut shape_candidates = Vec::new();
   212	        let mut statement_ends = Vec::new();
   213	        let mut failed = false;
   214	        let count = decl.body.len();
   215	        let arguments = binder.scopes.clone();
   216	        for (index, statement) in decl.body.iter().enumerate() {
   217	            if decl.kind == ProgramKind::Job {
   218	                binder.scopes = arguments.clone();
   219	                binder.push_scope();
   220	                binder.in_prelude = true;
   221	                let mut prelude = setup::Body::default();
   222	                for statement in &decl.prelude {
   223	                    if let Err(diagnostic) = binder.statement(statement, &mut prelude, false) {
   224	                        binder.record_error(diagnostic);
   225	                        binder.append_errors(diagnostics);
   226	                        return None;
   227	                    }
   228	                }
   229	                binder.in_prelude = false;
   230	                if !prelude.is_empty() {
   231	                    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.2 — each sweep carries its prelude as setup in the existing serialized statement vocabulary.
   232	                    body.push_if(Expr::Literal(Value::Bool(true)), prelude, setup::Body::default());
   233	                }
   234	            }
   235	            match binder.statement(statement, &mut body, index + 1 == count) {
   236	                Ok(()) => {
   237	                    if decl.kind == ProgramKind::Query && let S::Return { value: A::Shape { base, .. }, .. } = statement
   238	                        && matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Read(_) | A::Merge(_) | A::Name { .. }) {
   239	                        shape_candidates.push(body.len().saturating_sub(2));
   240	                    }
   241	                    // Binding a chunk prefix may hoist setup statements. The
   242	                    // chunk itself is the final statement emitted for this
   243	                    // declaration, so validate that scan directly instead of
   244	                    // zipping the differently-shaped AST and IR vectors.
   245	                    if decl.kind == ProgramKind::Job && let S::For { site, .. } = statement
   246	                        && !matches!(body.last(), Some(Stmt::For { domain: LoopDomain::Page(_), .. })) {
   247	                        diagnostics.push(placement(site, "a job sweep needs a table or prefix domain", "for row in table:"));

   249	                    }
   250	                    if decl.kind == ProgramKind::Job
   251	                        && let S::For { site, .. } = statement
   252	                        && let Some(Stmt::For { domain: LoopDomain::Page(scan), .. }) = body.last()
   253	                        && let Err(diagnostic) = stable_chunk(catalog, site, scan)

   255	                        diagnostics.push(diagnostic);
   256	                        failed = true;
   257	                    }
   258	                }
   259	                Err(diagnostic) => {
   260	                    binder.record_error(diagnostic);
   261	                    failed = true;
   262	                }
   263	            }

   265	            statement_ends.push(body.len());
   266	        }
   267	        if failed {
   268	            binder.append_errors(diagnostics);

   270	        }
   271	        // The interpreter returns null when control reaches the end. Include
   272	        // that observable path in the generated result type; a return nested
   273	        // in one branch or in a possibly-empty loop does not cover it.

   276	        }
   277	        let result = match binder.return_type() {
   278	            Ok(ty) => ty,
   279	            Err(error) => { diagnostics.push(error); return None; }
   280	        };
   281	        let full_name = super::operation_name(module, &decl.name);
   282	        let kind = match decl.kind {
   283	            ProgramKind::Query => ir::ProgramKind::Query,
   284	            ProgramKind::Mutation => ir::ProgramKind::Mutation,
   285	            ProgramKind::Job => ir::ProgramKind::Job,
   286	        };
   287	        let ownership = body.ownership;
   288	        let returned_shapes = returned_shapes::marks(&body.code, &full_name, &shape_candidates);
   289	        let program = Program { name: full_name, kind, args, read_time_granularity_ms: None, body: body.code, native: false, returned_shapes };
   290	        // @ref LLP 3000.036#17.8 — a total behind its grant's guard is the holder's whole total, as the server computes it.
   291	        let mut refused = false;
   292	        for (site, diagnostic) in std::mem::take(&mut binder.pending_totals) {
   293	            let guarded = ir::scans(&program).into_iter().filter(|scan| scan.site == site).all(|scan| snapback4_core::engine::rules::grant_dominated(schema, &program, scan));
   294	            if !guarded { diagnostics.push(diagnostic); refused = true; }
   295	        }
   296	        if refused { return None; }
   297	        let presence = if kind == ir::ProgramKind::Mutation {
   298	            Some(super::residency::presence_facts(schema, &program, &ownership, &binder.presence_sources))
   299	        } else { None };
   300	        Some(BoundProgram { maintenance_scopes: binder.maintenance_scopes, statement_ends, page_sources: binder.page_sources, canonical_sources: binder.canonical_sources, constraint_domains: binder.constraint_domains, filters: binder.filters, view_types: binder.view_types, selections: binder.selections, sources: binder.sources, receipt_sources: binder.receipt_sources, receipt_returns: binder.receipt_returns, implicit_write_results: binder.implicit_write_results, timeline_scans: binder.timeline_scans, program, presence, module: module.to_owned(), args: arg_types, result, notes: binder.notes, site: decl.site.clone(), job_bound_helpers: binder.job_bound_helpers })
   301	    }
   302	
   303	    fn site(&mut self) -> String {
   304	        self.sites += 1;
   305	        format!("programs.{}.{}", self.name, self.sites - 1)
   306	    }
   307	
   308	    fn temp(&mut self, hint: &str) -> String {
   309	        self.temps += 1;
   310	        format!("${hint}{}", self.temps)
   311	    }
   312	
   313	    fn lookup(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&Ty> {
   314	        self.binding(name).map(|binding| &binding.ty)
   315	    }
   316	
   317	    fn binding(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&Binding> {
   318	        self.scopes.iter().rev().find_map(|scope| scope.get(name))
   319	    }
   320	
   321	    fn declare(&mut self, name: &str, ty: Ty) -> String {
   322	        let runtime = if name.starts_with('$') { name.to_owned() } else { self.temp(name) };
   323	        self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name.to_owned(), Binding { runtime: runtime.clone(), declared: ty.clone(), ty, parameter: false, revision: 0, fields: BTreeMap::new(), error: None, origins: Vec::new() });
   324	        runtime
   325	    }
   326	
   327	    // @ref LLP 3000.015#3 — one maintenance type per helper declaration across all calls.
   328	    fn maintenance_local(&mut self, runtime: &str, site: &Site, name: &str) {
   329	        if self.depth > 0 {
   330	            self.maintenance_scopes.insert(runtime.into(), format!("helper/{}/local/{site}/{name}", self.call_chain.last().unwrap().0));
   331	        }
   332	    }
   333	
   334	    fn push_scope(&mut self) {
   335	        self.scopes.push(BTreeMap::new());
   336	    }
   337	
   338	    fn pop_scope(&mut self) {
   339	        self.scopes.pop();
   340	    }
   341	
   342	    fn table(&self, name: &str, site: &Site) -> Fallible<&'a TableInfo> {
   343	        if self.viewer_depth.is_some() && self.schema.table(name).is_some_and(|table| table.ephemeral.is_some()) {
   344	            return Err(job_reads::diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_QUERY", "job viewer reads require durable data", "keep ephemeral reads in a client query; persist the state the job needs"));
   345	        }
   346	        self.catalog.tables.get(name).ok_or_else(|| unknown(site, format!("unknown table {name}"), name_rewrite(name, self.catalog.tables.keys().map(String::as_str), None)))
   347	    }
   348	
   349	    // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#3-global-maintained-view-representation — every
   350	    // page admission path, including planned-view lanes, rejects the physical
   351	    // row behind a global maintained scalar.
   352	    pub(super) fn reject_global_scalar_page(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Fallible<()> {
   353	        if self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref())
   354	            .is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty())
   355	            && (!read.keys.is_empty() || read.range.is_some() || read.filter.is_some()
   356	                || read.matching.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() || !read.order_keys.is_empty())
   357	        {
   358	            return Err(type_error(&read.site, format!("{} is one global maintained scalar, not a group table", read.table),
   359	                format!("read it by its bare name: `return {}`", read.table)).guide("maintains"));
   360	        }
   361	        Ok(())
   362	    }
   363	
   364	    // ----- reads -----
   365	
   366	    fn read(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   367	        if read.matching.is_some() && self.binding(&read.table).is_some_and(|b| matches!(b.ty.required(), Ty::Text(_) | Ty::Enum(_))) {
   368	            return Err(type_error(&read.site, format!("`{}` is text; `matching` searches a table's word index", read.table),
   369	                format!("for text compare `{0} = q` or `{0} != q`; for whole-token membership use `q in tokens({0})`; for word-prefix row search write `<table> matching q first 50 by .id`", read.table)).guide("types"));
   370	        }
   371	        self.reject_global_scalar_page(read)?;
   372	        if let Some(timeline) = self.schema.timelines.iter().find(|timeline| timeline.name == read.table) {
   373	            let expanded = if let Some(plan) = self.catalog.feed_views.get(&read.table) {
   374	                super::feed_view::read(timeline, plan, read)?
   375	            } else { super::timeline::read(timeline, read)? };
   376	            // @ref LLP 3000.013#14.4 — the scale certificate trusts only the scan this expansion generates.
   377	            let outer = self.expanding_feed.replace((timeline.table("Feed"), read.site.clone()));
   378	            let bound = self.expr(&expanded, out);
   379	            self.expanding_feed = outer;
   380	            return bound;
   381	        }
   382	        if let Some(shaped) = self.page_shape(read) { return self.expr(&shaped, out); }
   383	        // @ref LLP 3000.036#11 — a bound row of this table addresses itself, as in `update t[row]`.
   384	        if let Some(by_id) = self.row_as_key(read) { return self.read(&by_id, out); }
   385	        if self.is_page(read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint)? {
   386	            let (scan, take) = self.scan(read, out)?;
   387	            return Ok((Expr::Scan(scan), Ty::Page(read.table.clone(), take)));
   388	        }
   389	        let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
   390	        if let Some(maintain) = &info.maintained {
   391	            if read.keys.len() != maintain.by.len() || read.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| key.column.as_ref()).any(|column| !maintain.by.contains(column)) {
   392	                return Err(super::maintains::refusal(&read.site, format!("{} scalar lookup needs the complete group key: {}", read.table, maintain.by.join(", ")), format!("supply every group key, or page the view: {} first 20 by .id after c", read.table)));
   393	            }
   394	        }
   395	        let value = self.read_value(read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint, out)?;
   396	        if let Some(maintain) = &info.maintained {
   397	            // A maintained point read is the typed value: zero when absent.
   398	            let row = self.temp("countRow");
   399	            let tmp = self.temp("total");
   400	            let zero = Expr::Literal(Value::Int(0));
   401	            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: row.clone(), value });
   402	            out.push(Stmt::If {
   403	                condition: Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left: Box::new(ir::var(&row)), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) },
   404	                then_body: vec![Stmt::Let { name: tmp.clone(), value: zero }],
   405	                else_body: vec![Stmt::Let { name: tmp.clone(), value: ir::field(ir::var(&row), "value") }],
   406	            });
   407	            let ty = info.columns.get("value").map(Ty::from_scalar).unwrap_or(Ty::Int);
   408	            let ty = self.maintained_result_type(maintain, read, ty);
   409	            return Ok((ir::var(&tmp), ty));
   410	        }
   411	        Ok((value, Ty::Row(read.table.clone()).optional()))
   412	    }
   413	
   414	    fn scan(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Scan, u32)> {
   415	        self.scan_at(read, out, prefix::Position::Page)
   416	    }
   417	
   418	    fn scan_at(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body, position: prefix::Position) -> Fallible<(Scan, u32)> {
   419	        self.reject_global_scalar_page(read)?;
   420	        let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
   421	        let (keys, index, selection) = self.select_index(info, read, ReadMode::Range, out)?;
   422	        let prefix = self.order_keys(info, &index, keys)?;
   423	        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5.1 — one pinned parent authorizes all of its aggregate groups.
   424	        if let Some(m) = self.schema.maintain_for(&read.table).filter(|m| m.groups_by_parent(self.schema)) {
   425	            let parent = self.schema.tables[&m.source].through.as_ref().unwrap();
   426	            if m.parent_key.is_none() || !index.columns.iter().take(prefix.len()).any(|f| f == &parent.reference) {
   427	                let fields = m.by.iter().filter(|f| *f != &parent.reference).map(|f| format!(".{f}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
   428	                let order = if fields.is_empty() { ".id" } else { &fields };
   429	                return Err(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_READ_THROUGH_SCAN",
   430	                    format!("{} must pin {} to one readable {} row before enumerating groups", read.table, parent.reference, parent.parent),
   431	                    format!("inside a page of {}, read `{}[{} = .id] by {order}` in its shape: `{{ *, totals: {}[{} = .id] by {order} }}`; keep the parent page's audience key, order and cursor. For a scalar supply every group key: {}[{}]. Omit an explicit aggregate read rule to inherit parent authorization", parent.parent, read.table, parent.reference, read.table, parent.reference, read.table, m.by.join(", "))).guide("maintains"));
   432	            }
   433	        }
   434	        let (range, bound) = self.prefix_bound(read, &index, position)?;
   435	        if info.maintained.is_some() && let Some(position) = index.columns.iter().position(|field| field == "value") {
   436	            if prefix.len() != position {
   437	                return Err(super::maintains::refusal(&range.site, format!("{} by {} needs every ranking scope field pinned: {}", read.table, index.name, index.columns[..position].join(", ")),
   438	                    format!("pin {} with where equalities, then read {} last {} by .value after c; omit the scope predicate for a global ranking", index.columns[..position].join(", "), read.table, range.take)));
   439	            }
   440	        }
   441	        if range.take == 0 {
   442	            return Err(type_error(&range.site, "a page holds at least one row", print::read_expr(&ReadExpr { range: Some(ast::RangeSpec { take: 50, ..range.clone() }), ..read.clone() })));
   443	        }
   444	        let mut page_source = read.clone();

   454	            key.column.get_or_insert_with(|| column.clone());
   455	        }
   456	        let filter_text = read.filter.as_ref().map(|expr| self.catalog.source.expression(expr));
   457	        let (read, filter) = self.scan_filter(read, &index)?;
   458	        let read = &read;
   459	        // Key expressions precede the seek bounds and cursor, including their setup.
   460	        let prefix: Vec<Expr> = prefix.into_iter().map(|value| self.snapshot(value, out)).collect();
   461	        let seek = self.seek(info, read, &index, out)?;
   462	        let mut after = None;
   463	        if let Some(cursor) = &range.cursor {
   464	            let (e, t) = self.expr(cursor, out)?;
   465	            if !matches!(t.required(), Ty::Cursor) {
   466	                return Err(type_error(cursor.site(), format!("after expects a cursor, got {}", t.name()), "declare c: cursor ? and pass after c"));
   467	            }
   468	            if !info.total_cursor(&index) {
   469	                let columns = index.columns.iter().map(|column| index.computed.get(column)
   470	                    .map(print::expression).unwrap_or_else(|| column.clone())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
   471	                if index.unique {
   472	                    // Do not add id to the unique constraint: that would
   473	                    // weaken it. Plan a separate pageable index from the read.
   474	                    let mut planned = read.clone();
   475	                    let rewrite = if index.computed.is_empty() {
   476	                        planned.index = None;
   477	                        for (key, column) in planned.keys.iter_mut().zip(&index.columns) { key.column.get_or_insert_with(|| column.clone()); }
   478	                        planned.order_keys = index.columns.iter().skip(prefix.len()).cloned().collect();
   479	                        print::read_expr(&planned)
   480	                    } else {
   481	                        let mut name = format!("{}Page", index.name);
   482	                        while info.indexes.iter().any(|index| index.name == name) { name.push('_'); }
   483	                        planned.index = Some(name.clone());
   484	                        format!("add `by {name}: {columns}, id` to {}; read `{}`", read.table, print::read_expr(&planned))
   485	                    };
   486	                    return Err(Diagnostic::new(&range.site, "E_CURSOR_INDEX", format!("index {}.{} cannot carry a cursor: its nullable unique key allows tied null rows without an id tiebreak", read.table, index.name), rewrite).guide("indexes"));
   487	                }
   488	                return Err(Diagnostic::new(&range.site, "E_CURSOR_INDEX", format!("index {}.{} cannot carry a cursor: it has no declared id or total unique key", read.table, index.name), format!("by {}: {columns}, id", index.name)).guide("indexes").mechanical());
   489	            }
   490	            after = Some(Box::new(e));
   491	        }
   492	        let mut prefix: Vec<IndexKeyExpr> = prefix.into_iter().map(IndexKeyExpr::Scalar).collect();
