   507	            format!(".{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value))
   508	        }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
   509	        let domain = if pins.is_empty() { read.table.clone() } else { format!("{} where {pins}", read.table) };
   510	        self.sources.insert(format!("domain/{scan_site}"), (read.site.clone(), domain));
   511	        if filter.is_some() && let Some(text) = filter_text { self.filters.insert(scan_site.clone(), text); }
   512	        // Only the generated scan itself, never an authored scan bound inside its keys, cursor or helpers.
   513	        if self.expanding_feed.as_ref().is_some_and(|(feed, site)| *feed == read.table && *site == read.site) { self.timeline_scans.insert(scan_site.clone()); }
   514	        self.record_selection(&scan_site, read, &index, if position == prefix::Position::Exists { "exists" } else { "page" }, &selection);
   515	        // Authored spelling serves diagnostics; inferred-index residency sites
   516	        // keep canonical bytes. Explicit-index search alternatives retain their
   517	        // authored origin even when presence planning synthesizes a browse index.
   518	        if !read.order_keys.is_empty() && let Some(text) = self.catalog.canonical_read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()) {
   519	            self.canonical_sources.insert(scan_site.clone(), text.clone());
   520	        }
   521	        let spelling = if !read.order_keys.is_empty() { self.catalog.read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()).cloned() } else { None };
   522	        self.sources.insert(scan_site.clone(), (read.site.clone(), spelling.unwrap_or_else(|| print::read_expr(&ReadExpr { index: None, range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new(), ..read.clone() }))));
   523	        let mut scan = Scan { cursor_pins: read.cursor_prefix.map_or(0, |old| (read.keys.len() - old) as u32),
   524	            variant: self.variant(),
   525	            site: scan_site,
   526	            table: read.table.clone(),
   527	            index: index.name,
   528	            prefix,
   529	            order: if range.order == ast::Order::Asc { Order::Asc } else { Order::Desc },
   530	            take: bound,
   531	            existence: position == prefix::Position::Exists,
   532	            after,
   533	            before: None,
   534	            range: seek,
   535	            filter,
   536	            opaque_cursor: range.cursor.is_some(),
   537	            merge_site: None,
   538	            provenance: KeyProvenance::Shape,
   539	        };
   540	        if position == prefix::Position::Aggregate && snapback4_core::engine::rules::scan_visibility(self.schema, &scan).uniform {
   541	            scan.take = Bound::Meter;
   542	        }
   543	        if matches!(position, prefix::Position::Exists | prefix::Position::Aggregate) {
   544	            self.constraint_domains.insert(scan.site.clone());
   545	        }
   546	        // @ref LLP 3000.028#4-put-the-rules-where-the-builder-meets-them — job/preview advice belongs only to a helper with a query-ineligible meter read.
   547	        if self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job && self.call_chain.len() > 1
   548	            && scan.take == Bound::Meter && !snapback4_core::engine::rules::scan_visibility(self.schema, &scan).uniform {
   549	            // Job domains always use the meter. A fitting pinned group cap instead
   550	            // gives a query a literal bound, bypassing its non-uniform meter refusal.
   551	            let cap = self.schema.group_bound(&scan.table, &index.columns[..read.keys.len()]);
   552	            if cap.is_none_or(|cap| u64::from(cap) > snapback4_core::Ceilings::default().examined) {
   553	                self.job_bound_helpers.insert(self.call_chain.last().unwrap().0.clone());
   554	            }
   555	        }
   556	        if self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Job && (position != prefix::Position::Aggregate || self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Query) { self.check_meter_prefix(read, &scan, position == prefix::Position::Aggregate)?; }
   557	        if position != prefix::Position::Aggregate { self.shape_meter_note(&scan, read); }
   558	        Ok((scan, range.take))
   559	    }
   560	
   561	    /// Evaluate the keys, then choose the index: explicit `by`, else by
   562	    /// key names, count and types. Point reads need a unique full key;
   563	    /// ranges need a strict prefix.
   564	    fn select_index(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, mode: ReadMode, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Vec<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>, ast::IndexDecl, String)> {
   565	        // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — retain bound keys for type selection and final lowering.
   566	        // No cloned expression pass: nested lookups must not multiply binder work.
   567	        let mut keys = Vec::<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>::new();
   568	        let mixed = read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_some()) && read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_none());
   569	        let types = if mixed {
   570	            let prefix = read.keys.iter().take_while(|key| key.column.is_none()).count();
   571	            self.bind_key_parts(info, read, prefix, &mut keys, out)?;
   572	            Some(keys.iter().map(|(_, expr, ty)| (ty.clone(), matches!(expr, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect::<Vec<_>>())
   573	        } else { None };
   574	        let mut normalized = super::mixed_keys::read_typed(info, read, self.catalog, types.as_deref())?;
   575	        self.name_bound_keys(info, &normalized, &mut keys)?;
   576	        let read = normalized.as_ref();
   577	        super::validate_key_names(read)?;
   578	        let mut positional_reason = None;
   579	        let mut positional_scan_repair = None;
   580	        if !read.keys.is_empty() && read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_none()) {
   581	            let declared = |index: &&ast::IndexDecl| !self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone()));
   582	            let mut candidates = info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).filter(|index| match &read.index {
   583	                Some(name) => &index.name == name,
   584	                None => index.unique && index.columns.len() == read.keys.len(),
   585	            }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   586	            let mut diagnostic_types = None;
   587	            if read.index.is_none() && candidates.len() > 1 {
   588	                self.bind_key_parts(info, read, read.keys.len(), &mut keys, out)?;
   589	                let types = keys.iter().map(|(_, expr, ty)| (ty.clone(), matches!(expr, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   590	                candidates = super::positional_keys::candidates(info, read, &types, &self.catalog.synthesized);
   591	                diagnostic_types = Some(types);
   592	            }
   593	            // A failed lookup may need operand types to identify a reference
   594	            // from the wrong table. Infer those on a discarded binder/body:
   595	            // successful reads retain the legacy contextual binding path and
   596	            // authored operands are still evaluated exactly once.
   597	            if candidates.is_empty() && diagnostic_types.is_none() {
   598	                let mut probe = self.clone();
   599	                let mut probe_keys = Vec::new();
   600	                let mut probe_out = setup::Body::default();
   601	                if probe.bind_key_parts(info, read, read.keys.len(), &mut probe_keys, &mut probe_out).is_ok() {
   602	                    diagnostic_types = Some(probe_keys.iter().map(|(_, expr, ty)| (ty.clone(), matches!(expr, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect());
   603	                }
   604	            }
   605	            if candidates.len() == 1 && !keys.is_empty() {
   606	                let mut selected = read.clone();
   607	                selected.index = Some(candidates[0].name.clone());
   608	                self.contextualize_bound_keys(info, &selected, &mut keys)?;
   609	            }
   610	            if read.index.is_none() && mode == ReadMode::Range && read.order_keys.is_empty() && candidates.len() == 1 {
   611	                let scan = info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).find(|index| !index.unique && index.is_plain() && index.columns.len() > read.keys.len());
   612	                let columns = scan.map(|index| index.columns.as_slice()).unwrap_or(&candidates[0].columns);
   613	                let predicate = read.keys.iter().zip(columns).map(|(key, column)| format!(".{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
   614	                positional_scan_repair = Some(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN", "a positional key selects one declared unique row, not a scan prefix",
   615	                    format!("{} where {predicate} first 20 by .id", read.table)).guide("reads"));
   616	            }
   617	            if candidates.len() != 1 {
   618	                return Err(super::positional_keys::refusal(info, read, diagnostic_types.as_deref().unwrap_or(&[]), &candidates, self.catalog));
   619	            }
   620	            if read.index.is_none() {
   621	                positional_reason = Some(if info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).filter(|index| index.unique && index.columns.len() == read.keys.len()).count() == 1 {
   622	                    "only declared unique key of this arity"
   623	                } else { "unique positional key selected by argument types" });
   624	                normalized.to_mut().index = Some(candidates[0].name.clone());
   625	            }
   626	        }
   627	        let read = normalized.as_ref();
   628	        let point = mode != ReadMode::Range;
   629	        self.bind_key_parts(info, read, read.keys.len(), &mut keys, out)?;
   630	        let named = keys.iter().all(|(c, _, _)| c.is_some()) && !keys.is_empty();
   631	        let mut selection = if read.index.is_some() { "explicit by" } else if named { "named equality columns" } else { "compatible positional key types" };
   632	        // A `words(..)` index serves one read: `matching` with exactly its
   633	        // leading columns as keys. Every other read sees plain indexes only.
   634	        let searching = mode == ReadMode::Range && read.matching.is_some();
   635	        let mut candidates: Vec<&ast::IndexDecl> = info
   636	            .indexes
   637	            .iter()
   638	            .filter(|index| read.index.as_ref().is_none_or(|explicit| explicit == &index.name))
   639	            .filter(|index| read.seeks.iter().all(|b| index.columns.get(keys.len()) == Some(&b.column)))
   640	            .filter(|index| if searching { index.searches_after(keys.len()) } else { index.words.is_none() })
   641	            .filter(|index| match mode {
   642	                ReadMode::Point | ReadMode::FilteredPoint => index.unique && (index.columns.len() == keys.len() || (named && mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.total_unique(index) && index.columns.len() < keys.len())),
   643	                ReadMode::Exists => (index.columns.len() == keys.len() && (named || read.index.is_some() || index.unique)) || (named && info.total_unique(index) && index.columns.len() < keys.len()),
   644	                ReadMode::Range => index.columns.len() > keys.len() || (!read.order_keys.is_empty() && index.unique && index.columns.len() == keys.len()),
   645	            })
   646	            .filter(|index| {
   647	                if named {
   648	                    if index.columns.len() < keys.len() { index.columns.iter().all(|column| keys.iter().any(|(c, _, _)| c.as_ref() == Some(column))) } else { keys.iter().all(|(c, _, _)| index.columns[..keys.len()].contains(c.as_ref().unwrap())) }
   649	                } else {
   650	                    true
   651	                }
   652	            })
   653	            .collect();
   654	        if named && matches!(mode, ReadMode::FilteredPoint | ReadMode::Exists) {
   655	            if let Some(width) = candidates.iter().filter(|i| info.total_unique(i)).map(|i| i.columns.len()).min() {
   656	                candidates.retain(|i| info.total_unique(i) && i.columns.len() == width);
   657	                // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.7 — named equalities covering two total unique keys
   658	                // (`loans[borrower = viewer, telescope = t]`, lane 057) test one row either
   659	                // way, the other key's columns being extra equalities on it; the first
   660	                // declared key decides, as a rule probe already does (§11).
   661	                // Same-column keys in two orders (`unique pair: low, high` and `unique
   662	                // inverse: high, low`) still need an explicit `by` (guide indexes).
   663	                let mut sets = candidates.iter().map(|index| { let mut columns = index.columns.clone(); columns.sort(); columns }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   664	                sets.dedup();
   665	                if candidates.len() > 1 && sets.len() == candidates.len() {
   666	                    selection = "first declared unique key the named equalities cover";
   667	                    candidates.truncate(1);
   668	                }
   669	            } else if candidates.iter().any(|i| i.unique) {
   670	                // Exact nullable unique keys still select points. A competing
   671	                // nonunique index must not turn an exists probe into a tie.
   672	                candidates.retain(|i| i.unique);
   673	            }
   674	        }
   675	        // Positional keys select by type; a text literal may meet an
   676	        // identity column (it becomes an id once the index is chosen).
   677	        if !named {
   678	            candidates.retain(|index| {
   679	                keys.iter().enumerate().all(|(position, (_, e, t))| {
   680	                    index.columns.get(position).is_some_and(|column| {
   681	                        let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, column);
   682	                        t.comparable(&expected) || (expected.is_identity() && matches!(e, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))
   683	                    })
   684	                })
   685	            });
   686	            // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.5 — resolve remaining ambiguity without replacing an existing exact-type winner.
   687	            let exact: Vec<_> = candidates.iter().copied().filter(|index| keys.iter().enumerate().all(|(position, (_, _, t))| index.columns.get(position).is_some_and(|column| self.key_ty(info, index, column) == t.required()))).collect();
   688	            if exact.len() == 1 {
   689	                candidates = exact;
   690	                if read.index.is_none() { selection = "single exact positional type match"; }
   691	            }
   692	            else {
   693	                // Filter before the one-candidate arm so identity literals receive their index's type.
   694	                let unique: Vec<_> = candidates.iter().copied().filter(|index| index.unique && index.columns.len() == keys.len()).collect();
   695	                if unique.len() == 1 {
   696	                    candidates = unique;
   697	                    if read.index.is_none() { selection = "only compatible full unique key"; }
   698	                }
   699	            }
   700	        }
   701	        // A single arity candidate follows the legacy bind-once path above.
   702	        // Only after that real binding proves the selected read impossible do
   703	        // its retained operand types refine the refusal's cause.
   704	        if candidates.is_empty() && positional_reason.is_some() && !named {
   705	            let types = keys.iter().map(|(_, expr, ty)| (ty.clone(), matches!(expr, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   706	            let mut implicit = read.clone();
   707	            implicit.index = None;
   708	            let compatible = super::positional_keys::candidates(info, &implicit, &types, &self.catalog.synthesized);
   709	            if compatible.is_empty() {
   710	                return Err(super::positional_keys::refusal(info, &implicit, &types, &compatible, self.catalog));
   711	            }
   712	            if let Some(repair) = positional_scan_repair {
   713	                return Err(repair);
   714	            }
   715	        }
   716	        if let Some(explicit) = &read.index
   717	            && candidates.is_empty()
   718	        {
   719	            let known = info.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == explicit);
   720	            return Err(match known {
   721	                None => unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no index {explicit}", read.table), name_rewrite(explicit, info.indexes.iter().map(|i| i.name.as_str()), None)),
   722	                Some(index) if index.words.is_some() && !searching => unknown(&read.site, format!("{}.{explicit} is a words index; it is read with matching", read.table), format!("{}[{}] matching q first 50 by {explicit}", read.table, index.columns[..index.words.as_ref().map_or(0, |(p, _)| *p)].join(", "))),
   723	                Some(index) if searching && index.words.is_none() => self.matching_nonwords_index(info, read, index),
   724	                Some(index) if searching => Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_MATCHING_INDEX", format!("{}.{explicit} has its words after {} column{}; matching takes exactly those as keys", read.table, index.words.as_ref().map_or(0, |(p, _)| *p), if index.words.as_ref().map_or(0, |(p, _)| *p) == 1 { "" } else { "s" }), format!("{}[{}] matching q first 50 by {explicit}", read.table, index.columns[..index.words.as_ref().map_or(0, |(p, _)| *p)].join(", "))),
   725	                Some(index) => self.index_mismatch(info, read, mode, &keys, index),
   726	            });
   727	        }
   728	        match candidates.len() {
   729	            1 => {
   730	                let index = candidates[0].clone();
   731	                if mode == ReadMode::Range && read.index.is_none() && !read.keys.is_empty() && !named {
   732	                    let predicate = read.keys.iter().zip(&index.columns).map(|(key, column)| format!(".{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
   733	                    let order = index.columns.iter().skip(read.keys.len()).map(|column| format!(".{column}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
   734	                    return Err(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN", format!("{} would select non-unique prefix {}", print::read_expr(read), index.name), format!("{} where {predicate} first 50 by {order}", read.table)).guide("reads"));
   735	                }
   736	                if !named {
   737	                    for (position, (_, e, t)) in keys.iter_mut().enumerate() {
   738	                        if let Some(column) = index.columns.get(position)
   739	                            && self.key_ty(info, &index, column).is_identity()
   740	                            && let Expr::Literal(Value::String(text)) = e
   741	                        {
   742	                            *e = Expr::Literal(Value::Id(text.clone()));
   743	                            *t = Ty::Id;
   744	                        }
   745	                    }
   746	                }
   747	                Ok((keys, index, positional_reason.unwrap_or(selection).into()))
   748	            }
   749	            0 => {
   750	                let declared: Vec<String> = info.indexes.iter().map(|i| format!("{} ({})", i.name, i.columns.join(", "))).collect();
   751	                if searching {
   752	                    let scope: Vec<String> = keys.iter().map(|(c, _, _)| c.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "<column>".into())).collect();
   753	                    Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no words index after {} key{}; its indexes are {}", read.table, keys.len(), if keys.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, declared.join(", ")), format!("by by{}Words: {}words(<text column>), at, id", scope.iter().map(|c| title(c)).collect::<String>(), scope.iter().map(|c| format!("{c}, ")).collect::<String>())))
   754	                } else if point {
   755	                    Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no unique index over {} column{} matching this key; its indexes are {}", read.table, keys.len(), if keys.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, declared.join(", ")), format!("unique by{}: {}", keys.iter().map(|(c, _, _)| c.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "Column".into())).map(|c| title(&c)).collect::<String>(), keys.iter().map(|(c, _, _)| c.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "<column>".into())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")) + if named && mode != ReadMode::Point { "  -- an index is needed when the named equalities do not cover a total unique key" } else { "" }))
   756	                } else {
   757	                    Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no index whose prefix takes {} key{}; its indexes are {}", read.table, keys.len(), if keys.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, declared.join(", ")), format!("by by{}: {}, id", keys.iter().map(|(c, _, _)| c.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "Column".into())).map(|c| title(&c)).collect::<String>(), keys.iter().map(|(c, _, _)| c.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "<column>".into())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))).guide("indexes"))
   758	                }
   759	            }
   760	            _ => {
   761	                let names: Vec<&str> = candidates.iter().map(|i| i.name.as_str()).collect();
   762	                let changes = candidates.iter().filter(|index| !self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())))
   763	                    .map(|index| crate::repair::ReadChange::Index(index.name.clone())).collect();
   764	                Err(ambiguous(&read.site, format!("{} has {} indexes matching this key: {}; choose an index valid for this read and its arguments", read.table, names.len(), names.join(", ")), "")
   765	                    .repair_read(&read.site, changes))
   766	            }
   767	        }
   768	    }
   769	
   770	    fn key_ty(&self, info: &TableInfo, index: &ast::IndexDecl, column: &str) -> Ty {
   771	        if index.computed.contains_key(column) { return Ty::Int; }
   772	        if column == "id" {
   773	            return Ty::Ref(info.decl.name.clone());
   774	        }
   775	        info.columns.get(column).map(Ty::from_scalar).unwrap_or(Ty::Unknown)
   776	    }
   777	
   778	    fn order_keys(&mut self, info: &TableInfo, index: &ast::IndexDecl, keys: Vec<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>) -> Fallible<Vec<Expr>> {
   779	        let named = keys.iter().all(|(c, _, _)| c.is_some());
   780	        if !named {
   781	            for (position, (_, _, t)) in keys.iter().enumerate() {
   782	                let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, &index.columns[position]);
   783	                if !t.comparable(&expected) {
   784	                    return Err(type_error(&index.site, format!("key {} is {}; {}.{}.{} is {}", position + 1, t.name(), info.decl.name, index.name, index.columns[position], expected.name()), format!("use {} for key {}", expected.name(), position + 1)));
   785	                }
   786	            }
   787	            return Ok(keys.into_iter().map(|(_, e, _)| e).collect());
   788	        }
   789	        let mut ordered = Vec::new();
   790	        for column in index.columns.iter().take(keys.len()) {
   791	            let Some(position) = keys.iter().position(|(c, _, _)| c.as_deref() == Some(column)) else {
   792	                return Err(unknown(&index.site, format!("key omits column {column} of {}.{}", info.decl.name, index.name), format!("name each prefix column once: {}", index.columns.iter().take(keys.len()).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))));
   793	            };
   794	            let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, column);
   795	            if !keys[position].2.comparable(&expected) {
   796	                return Err(type_error(&index.site, format!("{column} is {}; {}.{column} is {}", keys[position].2.name(), info.decl.name, expected.name()), format!("use {} for {column}", expected.name())));
   797	            }
   798	            ordered.push(keys[position].1.clone());
   799	        }
   800	        Ok(ordered)
   801	    }
   802	
   803	    fn walk(&mut self, table: &str, seed: &A, column: &str, depth: u32, site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Recurse, Ty)> {
   804	        let info = self.table(table, site)?;
   805	        let Some(next) = info.columns.get(column) else {
   806	            return Err(unknown(site, format!("{table} has no column {column}"), name_rewrite(column, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str), None)));
   807	        };
   808	        if !matches!(next.kind, ast::TypeKind::Ref { table: ref t } if t == table) {
   809	            return Err(type_error(site, format!("walk follows a reference back to {table}; {table}.{column} is {}", print::scalar_type(next)), format!("{column}: {table} ?")));
   810	        }
   811	        if depth == 0 || depth > 64 {
   812	            return Err(unbound_loop(site, "walk depth is between 1 and 64", format!("walk {table}[seed] up .{column} depth 8")));
   813	        }
   814	        let (seed_expr, _) = self.expr_as_reference(seed, out, &Ty::Ref(table.to_owned()))?;
   815	        Ok((Recurse { site: self.site(), table: table.to_owned(), index: "byId".into(), seed: Box::new(seed_expr), next_field: column.to_owned(), depth, fanout: 1, provenance: KeyProvenance::Computed }, Ty::Row(table.to_owned())))
   816	    }
   817	
   818	    fn merge(&mut self, merge: &ast::MergeExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   819	        if merge.lanes.iter().any(|lane| lane.planned.is_some()) || merge.index.is_empty() { return self.planned_view(merge, out, prefix::Position::Page); }
   820	        let info = self.table(&merge.table, &merge.site)?;
   821	        if merge.lanes.first().and_then(|lane| lane.table.as_deref()).is_some_and(|table| table != merge.table) {
   822	            return Err(type_error(&merge.site, "the first merge lane must use the header table, which owns the cursor", "start with [prefix] for the header table").guide("feeds"));
   823	        }
   824	        let Some(index) = info.indexes.iter().find(|index| index.name == merge.index).cloned() else {
   825	            return Err(unknown(&merge.site, format!("{} has no index {}", merge.table, merge.index), name_rewrite(&merge.index, info.indexes.iter().map(|i| i.name.as_str()), None)));
   826	        };
   827	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — matching merges lower to scoped matching scans in the existing lane IR.
   828	        let direction = if merge.order == ast::Order::Asc { "first" } else { "last" };
   829	        let matching_form = |scope: &[String]| format!("merge {} matching q{} {direction} {} by {}:\n  [{}]", merge.table, if merge.cursor.is_some() { " after c" } else { "" }, merge.take, merge.index, scope.join(", "));
   830	        if let Some((position, _)) = &index.words {
   831	            let scope = &index.columns[..*position];
   832	            if merge.matching.is_none() {
   833	                return Err(unsupported(&merge.site, "merge over a words index without matching", matching_form(scope)).guide("search"));
   834	            }
   835	            if merge.lanes.iter().any(|lane| lane.prefix.len() != *position || lane.columns.as_ref().is_some_and(|columns| columns.iter().flatten().any(|column| !scope.contains(column)))) {
   836	                return Err(type_error(&merge.site, format!("matching merge lanes must pin exactly the {} scope column(s) before words in {}.{}", position, merge.table, merge.index), matching_form(scope)).guide("search"));
   837	            }
   838	        } else if merge.matching.is_some() {
   839	            return Err(unsupported(&merge.site, "matching on a merge over a plain index", format!("merge {} {direction} {} by {}:\n  [prefix]  -- or merge {} matching q {direction} {} by <wordsIndex>:\n  [scope]", merge.table, merge.take, merge.index, merge.table, merge.take)).guide("search"));
   840	        }
   841	        let width = merge.lanes.first().map(|lane| lane.prefix.len()).unwrap_or(0);
   842	        if width >= index.columns.len() {
   843	            return Err(type_error(&merge.site, format!("merge lane prefixes must leave at least one {}.{} suffix column", merge.table, merge.index), "drop the last prefix key from every lane"));
   844	        }
   845	        if merge.lanes.iter().any(|lane| lane.prefix.len() != width) {
   846	            return Err(type_error(&merge.site, "merge lanes must have equal-width index prefixes", format!("use {width} prefix key(s) in every lane")));
   847	        }
   848	        let mut cursor_expr = None;
   849	        if let Some(cursor) = &merge.cursor {
   850	            let (e, t) = self.expr(cursor, out)?;
   851	            if !matches!(t.required(), Ty::Cursor) {
   852	                return Err(type_error(cursor.site(), format!("merge after expects a cursor, got {}", t.name()), "declare c: cursor ? and pass after c"));
   853	            }
   854	            cursor_expr = Some(e);
   855	        }
   856	        let merge_site = self.site();
   857	        let matching = merge.matching.as_ref().map(|value| self.expr(value, out)).transpose()?;
   858	        let matching = matching.map(|(value, ty)| {
   859	            let name = self.temp("matching");
   860	            let runtime = self.declare(&name, ty);
   861	            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: runtime, value });
   862	            Box::new(A::Name { name, site: merge.site.clone() })
   863	        });
   864	        let mut lanes = Vec::new();
   865	        for lane in &merge.lanes {
   866	            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.3 — inbox and public broadcast projections share a row and ordering, not an audience.
   867	            let table = lane.table.as_deref().unwrap_or(&merge.table);
   868	            if table != merge.table {
   869	                let other = self.table(table, &lane.site)?;
   870	                let other_index = other.indexes.iter().find(|i| i.name == merge.index);
   871	                if info.columns != other.columns || other_index.is_none_or(|i| i.columns != index.columns || i.unique != index.unique || i.words != index.words || self.schema.tables[table].indexes[&i.name] != self.schema.tables[&merge.table].indexes[&index.name]) || merge.matching.is_some() {
   872	                    return Err(type_error(&lane.site, "a named-table merge lane needs the same columns and plain index as its header table", format!("give {table} the columns and {} index of {}, or read it separately", merge.index, merge.table)).guide("feeds"));
   873	                }
   874	            }
   875	            self.push_scope();
   876	            let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
   877	            let mut source = None;
   878	            let mut binding = String::new();
   879	            if let Some((name, domain)) = &lane.loop_ {
   880	                // Source keys, seeks and predicate setup precede discovery; only
   881	                // the lane prefix may depend on the source row.
   882	                let (domain, ty) = self.lane_domain(domain, out)?;
   883	                source = Some(domain);
   884	                binding = self.declare(name, ty);
   885	                // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — filtered discovery lanes keep their selector in eager setup; lazy discovery freezes only prefixes.
   886	                if merge.filter.as_ref().is_some_and(|filter| source.as_ref().is_some_and(|source| source.scan().is_some()) || lanes::filter_uses_lane(filter, name)) {
   887	                    let source_binding = self.temp("source");
   888	                    setup.push(Stmt::Let { name: binding, value: ir::var(&source_binding) });
   889	                    binding = source_binding;
   890	                }
   891	            }
   892	            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — a merge predicate uses each lane's existing metered filter, before a candidate can be emitted.
   893	            let read = ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), order_keys: Vec::new(), table: table.to_owned(), keys: lane.prefix.iter().enumerate().map(|(position, value)| ast::KeyPart { column: lane.columns.as_ref().and_then(|c| c.get(position).cloned().flatten()), value: value.clone(), site: value.site().clone() }).collect(), index: Some(merge.index.clone()), range: Some(ast::RangeSpec { order: merge.order, take: merge.take, cursor: None, site: lane.site.clone() }), matching: matching.clone(), filter: merge.filter.clone(), seeks: Vec::new(), site: lane.site.clone() };
   894	            let (mut scan, _) = self.scan(&read, &mut setup)?;
   895	            self.pop_scope();
   896	            scan.after = cursor_expr.clone().map(Box::new);
   897	            scan.opaque_cursor = cursor_expr.is_some();
   898	            scan.merge_site = Some(merge_site.clone());
   899	            if let Some((_, domain)) = &lane.loop_ {
   900	                self.sources.insert(format!("for/{binding}"), (domain.site().clone(), self.catalog.source.expression(domain)));
   901	            }
   902	            lanes.push(MergeLane { guard: None, distinct: None, branch: None, project: None, setup: setup.code, scan, source, binding });
   903	        }
   904	        Ok((Expr::Merge(Merge { variant: self.variant(), position: None, view: None, order_keys: Vec::new(), site: merge_site, lanes }), Ty::Page(merge.table.clone(), merge.take)))
   905	    }
   906	
   907	    // ----- shapes -----
   908	
   909	    fn shape(&mut self, base: &A, fields: &[ShapeField], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   910	        let (base_expr, base_ty) = self.computation(base, out)?;
   911	        self.shape_bound(base_expr, base_ty, fields, site, out)
   912	    }
   913	
   914	    fn shape_bound(&mut self, base_expr: Expr, base_ty: Ty, fields: &[ShapeField], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   915	        match base_ty.clone() {
   916	            Ty::Page(table, max) => {
   917	                let list = self.temp("rows");
   918	                out.push(Stmt::Let { name: list.clone(), value: Expr::Array(Vec::new()) });
   919	                let item = self.temp("row");
   920	                let domain = match base_expr {
   921	                    Expr::Scan(scan) => LoopDomain::Page(scan),
   922	                    other => LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value: other, max, bound_source: "page".into() },
   923	                };
   924	                let mut body = setup::Body::default();
   925	                let (object, object_ty) = self.shape_object(&item, Ty::Row(table.clone()), fields, site, &mut body)?;
   926	                body.push(Stmt::Push { target: list.clone(), value: object });
   927	                out.push_for(item, domain, body);
   928	                Ok((ir::var(&list), Ty::Collection(Box::new(object_ty), max)))
   929	            }
   930	            Ty::Collection(item_ty, max) | Ty::List(item_ty, max) => {
   931	                let list = self.temp("items");
   932	                out.push(Stmt::Let { name: list.clone(), value: Expr::Array(Vec::new()) });
   933	                let item = self.temp("item");
   934	                let mut body = setup::Body::default();
   935	                let (object, object_ty) = self.shape_object(&item, *item_ty, fields, site, &mut body)?;
   936	                body.push(Stmt::Push { target: list.clone(), value: object });
   937	                out.push_for(item, LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value: base_expr, max, bound_source: "shape".into() }, body);
   938	                Ok((ir::var(&list), Ty::Collection(Box::new(object_ty), max)))
   939	            }
   940	            Ty::Row(_) | Ty::WriteRow(_) | Ty::Object(_) | Ty::RowShape(_) | Ty::RowObject(_) => {
   941	                let row = self.temp("row");
   942	                out.push(Stmt::Let { name: row.clone(), value: base_expr });
   943	                self.shape_object(&row, base_ty, fields, site, out)
   944	            }
   945	            Ty::Optional(inner) if matches!(*inner, Ty::Row(_) | Ty::WriteRow(_) | Ty::Object(_) | Ty::RowShape(_) | Ty::RowObject(_)) => {
   946	                let row = self.temp("row");
   947	                out.push(Stmt::Let { name: row.clone(), value: base_expr });
   948	                let result = self.temp("shaped");
   949	                out.push(Stmt::Let { name: result.clone(), value: Expr::Literal(Value::Null) });
   950	                let mut body = setup::Body::default();
   951	                let (object, object_ty) = self.shape_object(&row, *inner, fields, site, &mut body)?;
   952	                body.push(Stmt::Assign { name: result.clone(), value: object });
   953	                out.push_if(Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::var(&row)), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) }, body, setup::Body::default());
   954	                Ok((ir::var(&result), object_ty.optional()))
   955	            }
   956	            other => Err(type_error(site, format!("a shape applies to a row, an object or a page; got {}", other.name()), "shape a read: `messages[id] { body }`")),
   957	        }
   958	    }
   959	
   960	    /// Build the object of a shape over a focal row variable.
   961	    fn shape_object(&mut self, row_var: &str, row_ty: Ty, fields: &[ShapeField], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   962	        objects::explicit_fields(fields.iter().filter_map(|f| match f {
   963	            ShapeField::Bare { name, site } | ShapeField::Field { name, site, .. } => Some((name.as_str(), site)),
   964	            _ => None,
   965	        }))?;
   966	        let prior = self.focal.replace((row_var.to_owned(), row_ty.clone()));
   967	        self.push_scope();
   968	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#10 — the focal row is a lexical snapshot too;
   969	        // let its field guards carry presence facts into point-key arguments.
   970	        self.scopes.last_mut().unwrap().insert(row_var.into(), Binding { runtime: row_var.into(), declared: row_ty.clone(), ty: row_ty.clone(), parameter: false, revision: 0, fields: BTreeMap::new(), error: None, origins: Vec::new() });
   971	        let result = (|| {
   972	            let mut exprs = Vec::<(String, Expr)>::new();
   973	            let mut types = BTreeMap::new();
   974	            let mut add = |binder: &mut Self, name: String, e: Expr, t: Ty, setup: setup::Body, out: &mut setup::Body| {
   975	                if types.contains_key(&name) {
   976	                    // A replaced value still runs and pays its meter. Freeze
   977	                    // preceding fields before dropping one from the output,
   978	                    // including failures/reads with no setup statements.
   979	                    for (_, value) in &mut exprs {
   980	                        *value = binder.snapshot(std::mem::replace(value, Expr::Literal(Value::Null)), out);
   981	                    }
   982	                    exprs.retain(|(field, _)| field != &name);
   983	                }
   984	                binder.sequence_setup(exprs.iter_mut().map(|(_, e)| e), setup, out);
   985	                let e = if expr::allocates(&e) { binder.snapshot(e, out) } else { e };
   986	                exprs.push((name.clone(), e));
   987	                types.insert(name, t);
   988	            };
   989	            for field in fields {
   990	                match field {
   991	                    ShapeField::Spread { site } => {
   992	                        for (name, _) in self.row_fields(&row_ty, site)? {
   993	                            if matches!(row_ty, Ty::RowShape(_)) && fields.iter().any(|field| matches!(field, ShapeField::Field { name: explicit, .. } if explicit == &name)) { continue; }
   994	                            // @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — wildcard selection has the same
   995	                            // optional private-field semantics as explicit selection.
   996	                            let (value, ty) = self.member(ir::var(row_var), row_ty.clone(), &name, site)?;
   997	                            add(self, name, value, ty, setup::Body::default(), out);
   998	                        }
   999	                    }
  1000	                    ShapeField::NamedSpread { name, site } => {
  1001	                        let Some(shape) = self.catalog.shapes.get(name).cloned() else {
  1002	                            return Err(unknown(site, format!("unknown shape {name}"), name_rewrite(name, self.catalog.shapes.keys().map(String::as_str), None)));
  1003	                        };
  1004	                        let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
  1005	                        let (nested, nested_ty) = self.shape_object(row_var, row_ty.clone(), &shape.fields, site, &mut setup)?;
  1006	                        if let (Expr::Object(nested_fields) | Expr::Shape { fields: nested_fields, .. }, Ty::Object(nested_types) | Ty::RowShape(nested_types) | Ty::RowObject(nested_types)) = (nested, nested_ty) {
  1007	                            for (name, e) in nested_fields {
  1008	                                let t = nested_types[&name].clone();
  1009	                                add(self, name, e, t, std::mem::take(&mut setup), out);
  1010	                            }
  1011	                        }
  1012	                    }
  1013	                    ShapeField::Bare { name, site } => {
  1014	                        let (e, t) = self.member(ir::var(row_var), row_ty.clone(), name, site)?;
  1015	                        add(self, name.clone(), e, t, setup::Body::default(), out);
  1016	                    }
  1017	                    ShapeField::Field { name, value, site } => {
  1018	                        let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
  1019	                        let prior = self.shape_field.replace((name.clone(), site.clone()));
  1020	                        let result = self.expr(value, &mut setup);
  1021	                        self.shape_field = prior;
  1022	                        let (e, t) = result?;
  1023	                        add(self, name.clone(), e, t, setup, out);
  1024	                    }
  1025	                }
  1026	            }
  1027	            let _ = site;
  1028	            let fields = exprs.into_iter().collect();
  1029	            if matches!(row_ty, Ty::Row(_) | Ty::WriteRow(_) | Ty::RowShape(_) | Ty::RowObject(_)) {
  1030	                if types.contains_key("pending") { return Err(type_error(site, "pending is the device's row metadata", "omit pending from the shape; it is preserved from the row")); }
  1031	                let ty = if matches!(row_ty, Ty::RowObject(_)) { Ty::RowObject(types) } else { Ty::RowShape(types) };
  1032	                Ok((Expr::Shape { base: Box::new(ir::var(row_var)), fields }, ty))
  1033	            } else { Ok((Expr::Object(fields), Ty::Object(types))) }
  1034	        })();
  1035	        self.pop_scope();
  1036	        self.focal = prior;
  1037	        result
  1038	    }
  1039	
  1040	    fn row_fields(&self, ty: &Ty, site: &Site) -> Fallible<Vec<(String, Ty)>> {
  1041	        match ty.required() {
  1042	            Ty::WriteRow(table) => {
  1043	                let info = self.table(&table, site)?;
  1044	                let mut fields = vec![("id".to_owned(), Ty::Ref(table.clone()))];
  1045	                fields.extend(info.columns.iter().filter(|(name,_)| !super::columns::fields(info).any(|f|f == *name)).map(|(name,scalar)|(name.clone(),Ty::from_scalar(scalar))));
  1046	                Ok(fields)
  1047	            }
  1048	            Ty::Row(table) => {
  1049	                let info = self.table(&table, site)?;
  1050	                let mut fields = vec![("id".to_owned(), Ty::Ref(table.clone()))];
  1051	                fields.extend(info.columns.iter().map(|(name, scalar)| (name.clone(), Ty::from_scalar(scalar))));
  1052	                Ok(fields)
  1053	            }
  1054	            Ty::Object(fields) | Ty::RowShape(fields) | Ty::RowObject(fields) => Ok(fields.into_iter().collect()),
  1055	            other => Err(type_error(site, format!("* spreads a row or an object; got {}", other.name()), "shape a row")),
  1056	        }
  1057	    }
  1058	
  1059	    // ----- writes -----
  1060	
  1061	    /// Bind a write. When `valued`, the write yields its row (an insert's
  1062	    /// image, an update's or upsert's re-read row, null for a delete).
  1063	    fn write(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body, valued: bool) -> Fallible<Option<(Expr, Ty)>> {
  1064	        let result = self.write_inner(expr, out, valued);
  1065	        self.invalidate_after_write(expr);
  1066	        result
  1067	    }
  1068	
  1069	    fn write_inner(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body, valued: bool) -> Fallible<Option<(Expr, Ty)>> {
  1070	        if self.viewer_depth.is_some() { return Err(job_reads::read_only(expr.site())); }
  1071	        let A::Write { op, table, keys, fields, if_absent, condition, site } = expr else { unreachable!() };
  1072	        // @ref LLP 3000.036#12 — `delete row` and `update row { … }` write the
  1073	        // row a present row variable holds: the same write as `t[row]`.
  1074	        if keys.is_empty() && matches!(op, WriteOp::Delete | WriteOp::Update) {
  1075	            let resolved = match self.lookup(table) {
  1076	                Some(Ty::Row(target)) => Some(target.clone()),
  1077	                _ => None,
  1078	            };
  1079	            let Some(target) = resolved else {
  1080	                let (message, rewrite) = match self.lookup(table) {
  1081	                    Some(Ty::Optional(inner)) if matches!(inner.as_ref(), Ty::Row(_)) => {
  1082	                        let Ty::Row(target) = inner.as_ref() else { unreachable!() };
  1083	                        (format!("{table} may be null; {} needs a present row", op.word()), format!("{table} = {target}[id] ! else NOT_FOUND, then {} {table}", op.word()))
  1084	                    }
  1085	                    Some(other) => (format!("{} {table} needs a row variable; {table} is {}", op.word(), other.name()), format!("{} t[{table}]", op.word())),
  1086	                    None if self.catalog.tables.contains_key(table.as_str()) => (format!("{} {table} names no row", op.word()), format!("{} {table}[id]{}", op.word(), if *op == WriteOp::Update { " { … }" } else { "" })),
  1087	                    None => (format!("unknown row variable {table}"), format!("bind it first: row = t[id] ! else NOT_FOUND, then {} row", op.word())),
  1088	                };
  1089	                return Err(unknown(site, message, rewrite));
  1090	            };
  1091	            let key = ast::KeyPart { column: None, value: A::Name { name: table.clone(), site: site.clone() }, site: site.clone() };
  1092	            let resolved = A::Write { op: *op, table: target, keys: vec![key], fields: fields.clone(), if_absent: *if_absent, condition: condition.clone(), site: site.clone() };
  1093	            return self.write_inner(&resolved, out, valued);
  1094	        }
  1095	        if self.in_prelude {
  1096	            return Err(placement(site, "a write belongs to a chunk", format!("move {} {table} inside a top-level `for row in table:` job sweep", op.word())));
  1097	        }
  1098	        if self.kind == ProgramKind::Query {
  1099	            return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_PLACEMENT", format!("queries cannot {} {table}", op.word()), "move the write into a mutation").guide("programs"));
  1100	        }
  1101	        let info = self.table(table, site)?;
  1102	        if info.maintained.is_some() {
  1103	            return Err(unknown(site, format!("{table} is a maintained aggregate; write its source rows instead"), format!("{} {}", op.word(), info.maintained.as_ref().map(|m| m.source.clone()).unwrap_or_default())));
  1104	        }
  1105	        for field in fields {
  1106	            if info.decl.columns.iter().any(|c| c.name == field.name && c.sequence.is_some()) {
  1107	                return Err(Diagnostic::new(&field.site, "E_SEQUENCE_SUPPLIED", format!("{table}.{} is assigned by the engine", field.name),
  1108	                    format!("omit {} from the {} payload", field.name, op.word())).guide("writes"));
  1109	            }
  1110	            if super::columns::fields(info).any(|name|name == field.name) {
  1111	                return Err(super::columns::refusal(&field.site,format!("{table}.{} is engine-maintained; write its source rows instead",field.name)));
  1112	            }
  1113	        }
  1114	        self.system_principal_write(info, op, fields, *if_absent, site)?;
  1115	        self.note_denied_write(info, op, fields, site);
  1116	        // Writes share table-level residency facts, but every source occurrence
  1117	        // (including an expanded helper) belongs in a changed-prediction note.
  1118	        let write_name = format!("{} {table}", op.word());
  1119	        self.sources.insert(format!("write/{write_name}/{site}"), (site.clone(), write_name));
  1120	        match op {
  1121	            WriteOp::Insert => {
  1122	                let (row, row_ty) = self.write_row(info, fields, None, site, out)?;
  1123	                let tmp = self.temp("inserted");
  1124	                out.push(Stmt::Const { name: tmp.clone(), value: row });
  1125	                if *if_absent {
  1126	                    let result = self.temp("absent");
  1127	                    out.push(Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { table: table.clone(), row: ir::var(&tmp), result: result.clone() });
  1128	                    return Ok(valued.then(|| (self.project_insert(info, ir::var(&result)), row_ty.optional())));
  1129	                }
  1130	                out.push(Stmt::Insert { table: table.clone(), row: ir::var(&tmp) });
  1131	                Ok(valued.then(|| (self.project_insert(info, ir::var(&tmp)), row_ty)))
  1132	            }
  1133	            WriteOp::Update | WriteOp::Upsert => {
  1134	                // @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — upsert's insert arm has no old row on which to decide a condition.
  1135	                if *op == WriteOp::Upsert && condition.is_some() {
  1136	                    return Err(unknown(site, "upsert cannot take a condition", "a condition belongs on update; an upsert inserts or patches"));
  1137	                }
  1138	                // @ref LLP 3000.007#8 — item 7: target resolution and conditional old images belong to the engine.
  1139	                let key = self.write_key(info, keys, site, out).map_err(|error| self.extra_write_test(info, expr, error))?;
  1140	                let upsert = *op == WriteOp::Upsert;
  1141	                if upsert && fields.iter().any(|f| f.name == "id") {
  1142	                    return Err(unknown(site, "upsert cannot spell id; the key names the row", "remove id from the patch; the key names the row"));
  1143	                }
  1144	                let (patch, _) = self.write_row(info, fields, Some("patch"), site, out)?;
  1145	                let condition = if let Some(condition) = condition {
  1146	                    let old = self.temp("condition");
  1147	                    let prior = self.focal.replace((old.clone(), Ty::Row(table.clone())));
  1148	                    let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
  1149	                    let bound = self.expr(condition, &mut setup);
  1150	                    self.focal = prior;
  1151	                    let (value, ty) = bound?;
  1152	                    if !ty.is_bool() {

  1181	    /// `t[row]` where `row` is a present row of `t`: the same read as `t[row.id]`.
  1182	    fn row_as_key(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<ReadExpr> {

  1193	    fn write_key(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, keys: &[ast::KeyPart], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<WriteKey> {

  1219	        }

  1223	    fn write_row(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, fields: &[ObjectField], current: Option<&str>, site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {

  1274	    fn write_row_with_id(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, fields: &[ObjectField], id: Expr, site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {

  1319	    }

  1331	    fn calendar_number(&self, value: &A, depth: u8) -> bool {
  1332	        if depth > 8 { return false; }
  1333	        match value {
  1334	            A::Call { name, .. } => matches!(name.as_str(), "day" | "hour" | "bucket"),
  1335	            A::Name { name, .. } => self.binding(name).is_some_and(|binding| binding.revision == 0 && binding.origins.len() == 1 && self.calendar_number(&binding.origins[0].1, depth + 1)),

  1337	            _ => false,
  1338	        }
  1339	    }

  1341	    fn field_value(&mut self, field: &ObjectField, expected: &Ty, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {

  1386	/// A constant written into a bounded int column is checked where it is written;
  1387	/// a computed value meets the bound at run time (E_INPUT).
  1388	// @ref LLP 3000.040#14-where-the-bound-is-enforced — the binder refuses what the runtime would always refuse.
  1389	pub(super) fn literal_in_range(info: &TableInfo, column: &str, scalar: &crate::ast::ScalarType, value: &Expr, site: &Site) -> Fallible<()> {
  1390	    let constant = match value {
  1391	        Expr::Literal(Value::Int(n)) => Some(*n),
  1392	        Expr::Unary { op: snapback4_core::ir::UnaryOp::Neg, value } => match value.as_ref() { Expr::Literal(Value::Int(n)) => Some(-n), _ => None },
  1393	        _ => None,
  1394	    };
  1395	    if let (crate::ast::TypeKind::IntRange { min, max }, Some(n)) = (&scalar.kind, constant)
  1396	        && !snapback4_core::schema::int_in_range(n, *min, *max) {
  1397	        let range = snapback4_core::schema::int_range_text(*min, *max);
  1398	        return Err(type_error(site, format!("{}.{column} is {range}; {n} is outside it", info.decl.name), format!("write a value in {range}, or widen the column's range")).guide("types"));
  1399	    }
  1400	    Ok(())
  1401	}
  1402	
  1403	fn literal_int(expr: &A) -> Option<i64> {
  1404	    match expr {
  1405	        A::Literal { value: Literal::Number(text), .. } => text.parse().ok(),
  1406	        A::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "-" => literal_int(value).map(|n| -n),
  1407	        _ => None,
  1408	    }
  1409	}

  1411	pub(super) fn validate_code(site: &Site, code: &str) -> Fallible<()> {
  1412	    if code.starts_with("E_") {
  1413	        return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_OP_RESERVED", format!("application refusal code {code} uses the reserved E_ namespace"), code.trim_start_matches("E_").to_owned()).guide("programs"));
  1414	    }
  1415	    if !code.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_uppercase() || b.is_ascii_digit() || b == b'_') {
  1416	        return Err(unknown(site, format!("refusal codes are UPPER_SNAKE words; {code} is not"), code.to_uppercase().replace(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_alphanumeric(), "_")));
  1417	    }
  1418	    Ok(())
  1419	}

  1421	fn title(value: &str) -> String {
  1422	    let mut chars = value.chars();
  1423	    match chars.next() {
  1424	        Some(first) => first.to_uppercase().collect::<String>() + chars.as_str(),
  1425	        None => String::new(),
  1426	    }
  1427	}

  1429	/// A sweep continues across commits from a cursor, so no write may move an
  1430	/// unvisited row across it: every column of the chunk's index is `id` or
  1431	/// declared `immutable` (LLP 1016 §4 D2).
  1432	fn stable_chunk(catalog: &Catalog, site: &Site, scan: &Scan) -> Fallible<()> {
  1433	    let Some(info) = catalog.tables.get(&scan.table) else { return Ok(()) };
  1434	    let Some(index) = info.indexes.iter().find(|index| index.name == scan.index) else { return Ok(()) };
  1435	    let immutable: Vec<&str> = info.decl.immutable.iter().flat_map(|(columns, _)| columns.iter().map(String::as_str)).collect();
  1436	    let moving: Vec<&str> = index.columns.iter().map(|column| match index.computed.get(column) {
  1437	        Some(A::Call { args, .. }) => match args.first() { Some(A::Name { name, .. }) => name.as_str(), _ => column.as_str() },
  1438	        _ => column.as_str(),
  1439	    }).filter(|column| *column != "id" && !immutable.contains(column)).fold(Vec::new(), |mut columns, column| {
  1440	        if !columns.contains(&column) { columns.push(column); }
  1441	        columns
  1442	    });
  1443	    if !moving.is_empty() {
  1444	        return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_JOB_CURSOR_INDEX", format!("sweep over {}.{} sweeps by {}, which a write could change under the cursor; a sweep's index columns are id or immutable", scan.table, scan.index, moving.join(", ")), format!("use `for row in {}:` for stable id order; if this order is required, declare `immutable {}` on table {}", scan.table, moving.join(", "), scan.table)).guide("jobs"));
  1445	    }
  1446	    Ok(())
  1447	}

     1	//! Null facts for repeated, effect-free helper calls over lexical snapshots.
     2	use super::*;
     3	use std::collections::BTreeSet;
     4	
     5	pub(super) const CONSTANTS: &str = "$pureCallConstants";
     6	
     7	#[derive(Debug)]
     8	pub(super) enum Stable {
     9	    Literal(String),
    10	    Snapshot(String, Vec<String>),
    11	    Viewer,
    12	    Call(String, Vec<Stable>),

    14	
    15	impl Binder<'_> {
    16	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.2 — a repeated pure call narrows like a snapshot field.
    17	    // Facts live on *every* argument root so the existing branch intersections,
    18	    // reassignment invalidation and loop fixed point apply to them as to fields.
    19	    fn stable_call(&self, value: &A) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
    20	        if !matches!(value, A::Call { .. }) { return None; }
    21	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
    22	        let stable = self.stable_value(value, &mut roots)?;
    23	        if roots.is_empty() { roots.insert(CONSTANTS.into()); }
    24	        Some((vec!["$pureCall".into(), stable.key()], roots))
    25	    }
    26	
    27	    pub(super) fn stable_value(&self, value: &A, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<Stable> {
    28	        match value {
    29	            A::Literal { .. } => Some(Stable::Literal(print::expression(value))),
    30	            A::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer" => Some(Stable::Viewer),
    31	            A::Name { name, .. } if matches!(name.as_str(), "now" | "commit" | "new") => None,
    32	            A::Name { .. } | A::Member { .. } | A::Focal { .. } => {
    33	                let (root, path) = control::field_path(value, self.focal.as_ref().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()))?;
    34	                let runtime = if let Some(binding) = self.binding(&root) {
    35	                    roots.insert(root.clone());
    36	                    binding.runtime.clone()
    37	                } else {
    38	                    // A shape's implicit row is an immutable lexical snapshot.
    39	                    // Its unique runtime identity separates projection scopes;
    40	                    // with no mutable argument roots, use the constant anchor.
    41	                    self.focal.as_ref().filter(|(name, _)| name == &root)?.0.clone()
    42	                };
    43	                Some(Stable::Snapshot(runtime, path))
    44	            }
    45	            A::Call { name, args, .. } => {
    46	                let function = self.catalog.functions.get(name)?;
    47	                if !self.pure_functions.get(&function.name).copied().unwrap_or(false) { return None; }
    48	                Some(Stable::Call(function.name.clone(), args.iter().map(|arg| self.stable_value(arg, roots)).collect::<Option<_>>()?))
    49	            }
    50	            _ => None,
    51	        }
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    pub(super) fn narrow_call(&mut self, value: &A, present: bool) {
    55	        let Some((key, roots)) = self.stable_call(value) else { return; };
    56	        let Some(ty) = self.call_types.get(&key) else { return; };
    57	        let ty = if present { ty.required() } else { Ty::Null };
    58	        for root in roots {
    59	            let mut binding = self.binding(&root).expect("a stable root").clone();
    60	            binding.fields.insert(key.clone(), ty.clone());
    61	            self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(root, binding);
    62	        }
    63	    }
    64	
    65	    pub(super) fn record_call(&mut self, value: &A, ty: &Ty) {
    66	        if let Some((key, _)) = self.stable_call(value) { self.call_types.insert(key, ty.clone()); }
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    pub(super) fn call_refinement(&self, value: &A) -> Option<Ty> {
    70	        let (key, roots) = self.stable_call(value)?;
    71	        let mut result = None;
    72	        for root in roots {
    73	            let ty = self.binding(&root)?.fields.get(&key)?;
    74	            if result.as_ref().is_some_and(|other| other != ty) { return None; }
    75	            result = Some(ty.clone());
    76	        }
    77	        result
    78	    }
    79	}

    81	impl Stable {
    82	    // Escaped serialization of the structured identity, never source
    83	    // sites or helper temporaries. Argument order and snapshot identity matter.
    84	    pub(super) fn key(&self) -> String {
    85	        match self {
    86	            Self::Literal(value) => format!("literal:{value:?}"),
    87	            Self::Snapshot(root, path) => format!("snapshot:{root:?}:{path:?}"),
    88	            Self::Viewer => "viewer".into(),
    89	            Self::Call(name, args) => format!("call:{name:?}:{:?}", args.iter().map(Self::key).collect::<Vec<_>>()),
    90	        }
    91	    }
    92	}
    93	
    94	// Inspect expanded IR, after argument setup: this covers transitive helpers,
    95	// named shapes, aggregates and reads hidden in blocks without a second AST
    96	// effect model. It only classifies effects; calls still execute and are metered.
    97	pub(super) fn expression(value: &Expr) -> bool {
    98	    match value {
    99	        Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Variable(_) | Expr::Input(Input::Viewer) => true,
   100	        Expr::Input(Input::Now | Input::ReadTime | Input::NewId) => false,
   101	        Expr::Get(_) | Expr::Exists(_) | Expr::Scan(_) | Expr::Merge(_) => false,
   102	        Expr::Field { base, .. } | Expr::Unary { value: base, .. } => expression(base),
   103	        Expr::Array(values) => values.iter().all(expression),
   104	        Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().all(expression),
   105	        Expr::Shape { base, fields } => expression(base) && fields.values().all(expression),
   106	        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => expression(left) && expression(right),
   107	        Expr::Builtin { builtin, args } => builtin_pure(builtin) && args.iter().all(expression),
   108	        Expr::Block { body, value } => statements(body) && expression(value),
   109	    }
   110	}
   111	
   112	fn builtin_pure(builtin: &Builtin) -> bool {
   113	    match builtin {
   114	        Builtin::RequireUncapped => false,
   115	        Builtin::JobAs | Builtin::JobQuery { .. } => false,
   116	        Builtin::Connected | Builtin::ReceiptSettled { .. } => false,
   117	        Builtin::ProjectFields { .. } | Builtin::OptionalField { .. } | Builtin::Calendar { .. } | Builtin::Civil(_)
   118	        | Builtin::Len | Builtin::Slice | Builtin::Concat | Builtin::Lower | Builtin::Trim
   119	        | Builtin::Sha256 | Builtin::Join | Builtin::Group { .. } | Builtin::First | Builtin::Last
   120	        | Builtin::At | Builtin::Min | Builtin::Max | Builtin::Extremum { .. }
   121	        | Builtin::ExtremumOf { .. } | Builtin::Divmod { .. } | Builtin::Divide { .. } | Builtin::Sort { .. }
   122	        | Builtin::MentionTokens | Builtin::SearchPresent | Builtin::DeterministicShare
   123	        | Builtin::Count | Builtin::Sum | Builtin::CountCapped => true,
   124	    }
   125	}
   126	
   127	pub(super) fn statements(body: &[Stmt]) -> bool {
   128	    body.iter().all(|statement| match statement {
   129	        Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. }
   130	        | Stmt::Push { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expression(value),
   131	        Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expression(condition),
   132	        Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => expression(condition) && statements(then_body) && statements(else_body),
   133	        Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => {
   134	            let pure = match domain {
   135	                LoopDomain::LiteralRange { .. } | LoopDomain::ArgList { .. } => true,
   136	                LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => expression(start) && expression(end_exclusive),
   137	                LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, .. } => expression(value),
   138	                LoopDomain::Page(_) | LoopDomain::Recurse(_) => false,
   139	            };
   140	            pure && statements(body)
   141	        }
   142	        Stmt::Break | Stmt::Continue => true,
   143	        Stmt::Check { .. } | Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { .. } | Stmt::Insert { .. }
   144	        | Stmt::Update { .. } | Stmt::Upsert { .. } | Stmt::WriteTarget { .. }
   145	        | Stmt::Delete { .. } | Stmt::DeleteTarget { .. } | Stmt::Emit { .. } => false,
   146	    })
   147	}

   770	    fn key_ty(&self, info: &TableInfo, index: &ast::IndexDecl, column: &str) -> Ty {
   771	        if index.computed.contains_key(column) { return Ty::Int; }
   772	        if column == "id" {
   773	            return Ty::Ref(info.decl.name.clone());
   774	        }
   775	        info.columns.get(column).map(Ty::from_scalar).unwrap_or(Ty::Unknown)
   776	    }
   777	
   778	    fn order_keys(&mut self, info: &TableInfo, index: &ast::IndexDecl, keys: Vec<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>) -> Fallible<Vec<Expr>> {
   779	        let named = keys.iter().all(|(c, _, _)| c.is_some());
   780	        if !named {
   781	            for (position, (_, _, t)) in keys.iter().enumerate() {
   782	                let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, &index.columns[position]);
   783	                if !t.comparable(&expected) {
   784	                    return Err(type_error(&index.site, format!("key {} is {}; {}.{}.{} is {}", position + 1, t.name(), info.decl.name, index.name, index.columns[position], expected.name()), format!("use {} for key {}", expected.name(), position + 1)));
   785	                }
   786	            }
   787	            return Ok(keys.into_iter().map(|(_, e, _)| e).collect());
   788	        }
   789	        let mut ordered = Vec::new();
   790	        for column in index.columns.iter().take(keys.len()) {
   791	            let Some(position) = keys.iter().position(|(c, _, _)| c.as_deref() == Some(column)) else {
   792	                return Err(unknown(&index.site, format!("key omits column {column} of {}.{}", info.decl.name, index.name), format!("name each prefix column once: {}", index.columns.iter().take(keys.len()).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))));
   793	            };
   794	            let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, column);
   795	            if !keys[position].2.comparable(&expected) {
   796	                return Err(type_error(&index.site, format!("{column} is {}; {}.{column} is {}", keys[position].2.name(), info.decl.name, expected.name()), format!("use {} for {column}", expected.name())));
   797	            }
   798	            ordered.push(keys[position].1.clone());
   799	        }
   800	        Ok(ordered)
   801	    }

   803	    fn walk(&mut self, table: &str, seed: &A, column: &str, depth: u32, site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Recurse, Ty)> {
   804	        let info = self.table(table, site)?;
   805	        let Some(next) = info.columns.get(column) else {
   806	            return Err(unknown(site, format!("{table} has no column {column}"), name_rewrite(column, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str), None)));
   807	        };
   808	        if !matches!(next.kind, ast::TypeKind::Ref { table: ref t } if t == table) {
   809	            return Err(type_error(site, format!("walk follows a reference back to {table}; {table}.{column} is {}", print::scalar_type(next)), format!("{column}: {table} ?")));
   810	        }
   811	        if depth == 0 || depth > 64 {
   812	            return Err(unbound_loop(site, "walk depth is between 1 and 64", format!("walk {table}[seed] up .{column} depth 8")));
   813	        }
   814	        let (seed_expr, _) = self.expr_as_reference(seed, out, &Ty::Ref(table.to_owned()))?;
   815	        Ok((Recurse { site: self.site(), table: table.to_owned(), index: "byId".into(), seed: Box::new(seed_expr), next_field: column.to_owned(), depth, fanout: 1, provenance: KeyProvenance::Computed }, Ty::Row(table.to_owned())))
   816	    }

     5	impl Binder<'_> {
     6	    // ----- statements -----
     7	
     8	    pub(super) fn block(&mut self, statements: &[S], out: &mut setup::Body, completes: bool) -> Fallible<()> {
     9	        let before = self.visible_bindings();
    10	        self.push_scope();
    11	        let count = statements.len();
    12	        let mut result = Ok(());
    13	        for (index, statement) in statements.iter().enumerate() {
    14	            if let Err(error) = self.statement(statement, out, completes && index + 1 == count) {
    15	                if result.is_ok() { result = Err(error); }
    16	            }
    17	        }
    18	        let after = self.visible_bindings();
    19	        self.pop_scope();
    20	        for (name, mut old) in before {
    21	            let new = &after[&name];
    22	            if old.runtime == new.runtime && old.revision != new.revision {
    23	                self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name, new.clone());
    24	            } else if old.runtime == new.runtime {
    25	                // Facts added inside a lexical block remain local, while a
    26	                // write's invalidation must reach the enclosing loop header.
    27	                let surviving_fields: BTreeMap<_, _> = old.fields.iter()
    28	                    .filter(|(path, ty)| new.fields.get(*path) == Some(*ty))
    29	                    .map(|(path, ty)| (path.clone(), ty.clone()))
    30	                    .collect();
    31	                if surviving_fields.len() != old.fields.len() {
    32	                    old.fields = surviving_fields;
    33	                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name, old);
    34	                }
    35	            }
    36	        }
    37	        result
    38	    }

    40	    pub(super) fn append_errors(&mut self, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    41	        for error in self.errors.drain(..) {
    42	            if !diagnostics.contains(&error) { diagnostics.push(error); }
    43	        }
    44	    }

    46	    pub(super) fn record_error(&mut self, error: Diagnostic) {
    47	        if !self.errors.contains(&error) { self.errors.push(error); }
    48	    }
    49	
    50	    pub(super) fn statement(&mut self, statement: &S, out: &mut setup::Body, completes: bool) -> Fallible<()> {
    51	        let result = self.statement_inner(statement, out, completes);
    52	        if let Err(error) = &result {
    53	            self.record_error(error.clone());
    54	            if let S::Bind { names, .. } = statement {
    55	                for name in names {
    56	                    if self.binding(name).is_some_and(|binding| binding.parameter) { continue; }
    57	                    let runtime = self.declare(name, Ty::Unknown);
    58	                    let binding = self.scopes.last_mut().unwrap().get_mut(name).unwrap();
    59	                    binding.error = Some(error.clone());
    60	                    out.push(Stmt::Let { name: runtime, value: Expr::Literal(Value::Null) });
    61	                }
    62	            }
    63	        }
    64	        result
    65	    }

   324	    pub(super) fn tuple_bind(&mut self, names: &[String], value: &A, site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<()> {
   325	        let A::Call { name, args, .. } = value else {
   326	            return Err(unknown(site, "tuple bindings take exactly two names and sorted(a, b) or divmod(a, b)", "(quotient, remainder) = divmod(dividend, divisor)"));
   327	        };
   328	        if args.len() != 2 || (name != "divmod" && name != "sorted") {
   329	            return Err(unknown(site, "tuple bindings take exactly two names and sorted(a, b) or divmod(a, b)", format!("({}, {}) = divmod(a, b)", names[0], names[1])));
   330	        }
   331	        for (position, local) in names.iter().enumerate() {
   332	            if names[..position].contains(local) || self.scopes.last().is_some_and(|scope| scope.contains_key(local)) || self.scopes[0].contains_key(local) {
   333	                return Err(unknown(site, format!("duplicate local {local}"), "give each tuple component a fresh name"));
   334	            }
   335	        }
   336	        let (a, at) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   337	        let ta = self.temp("a");
   338	        out.push(Stmt::Let { name: ta.clone(), value: a });
   339	        let (b, bt) = self.expr(&args[1], out)?;
   340	        if name == "divmod" {
   341	            if !at.is_numeric() || !bt.is_numeric() {
   342	                return Err(type_error(site, format!("divmod takes two present numbers, got {} and {}", at.name(), bt.name()), "narrow both operands first"));
   343	            }
   344	            let tmp = self.temp("divmod");
   345	            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: tmp.clone(), value: Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Divmod { remainder_sink: "lexicographic-id".into() }, args: vec![ir::var(&ta), b] } });
   346	            let first = self.declare(&names[0], Ty::Int);
   347	            let second = self.declare(&names[1], Ty::Int);
   348	            self.maintenance_local(&first, site, &names[0]);
   349	            self.maintenance_local(&second, site, &names[1]);
