     1	//! Presence facts for identical point reads across proved stable transaction states.
     2	use super::*;
     3	use std::collections::BTreeSet;
     4	const POINT: &str = "$point";
     5	
     6	impl Binder<'_> {
     7	    // A compound condition cannot prove an earlier read after a later effect.
     8	    // Unknown/table-reading helpers are conservative here; direct guards need none.
     9	    pub(super) fn point_guard_safe(&self, value: &A) -> bool {
    10	        match value {
    11	            A::Name { .. } | A::Focal { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => true,
    12	            A::Member { base, .. } | A::Unary { value: base, .. } | A::Assert { value: base, .. } => self.point_guard_safe(base),
    13	            A::Binary { left, right, .. } => self.point_guard_safe(left) && self.point_guard_safe(right),
    14	            A::Call { name, args, .. } => self.catalog.functions.get(name).is_none_or(|f| self.pure_functions.get(&f.name) == Some(&true))
    15	                && args.iter().all(|a| self.point_guard_safe(a)),
    16	            A::Read(read) | A::Exists { read, .. } => read.keys.iter().all(|k| self.point_guard_safe(&k.value)) && read.filter.is_none() && read.matching.is_none() && read.range.is_none(),
    17	            _ => false,
    18	        }
    19	    }
    20	    fn point_key(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
    21	        if read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() || read.filter.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() { return None; }
    22	        let info = self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table)?;
    23	        if info.maintained.is_some() || info.decl.ephemeral.is_some() { return None; }
    24	        let mut binder = self.clone();
    25	        let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
    26	        let (bound, index, _) = binder.select_index(info, read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint, &mut setup).ok()?;
    27	        // Exists over a nonunique key does not prove a point lookup.
    28	        if !index.unique || bound.len() != index.columns.len() { return None; }
    29	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::from([pure_calls::CONSTANTS.to_owned()]);
    30	        let mut key = vec![POINT.into(), read.table.clone(), index.name];
    31	        for part in &read.keys {
    32	            key.push(format!("{:?}:{}", part.column, self.stable_value(&part.value, &mut roots)?.key()));
    33	        }
    34	        Some((key, roots))
    35	    }
    36	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.2 — key roots and the transaction-state anchor own
    37	    // facts, so lexical joins, rebindings and loop fixed points invalidate them.
    38	    pub(super) fn narrow_point(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr) {
    39	        let Some((key, roots)) = self.point_key(read) else { return; };
    40	        for root in roots {
    41	            let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&root).cloned() else { return; };
    42	            binding.fields.insert(key.clone(), Ty::Bool);
    43	            self.scopes.last_mut().unwrap().insert(root, binding);
    44	        }
    45	    }
    46	    // @ref LLP 3000.013#14.7 — the runner buffers page domains before bodies.
    47	    /// A row from a freshly read, wholly write-free table domain is an exact
    48	    /// live point throughout the loop. Store it in the ordinary point-fact
    49	    /// domain; buffered or writeful loops must establish a current witness.
    50	    pub(super) fn narrow_fresh_row(&mut self, name: &str, ty: &Ty, site: &Site) {
    51	        let Ty::Row(table) = ty.required() else { return };
    52	        let value = A::Member {
    53	            base: Box::new(A::Name { name: name.into(), site: site.clone() }),
    54	            name: "id".into(),
    55	            site: site.clone(),
    56	        };
    57	        self.narrow_point(&ReadExpr {
    58	            cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), order_keys: Vec::new(), table,
    59	            keys: vec![ast::KeyPart { column: None, value, site: site.clone() }], seeks: Vec::new(), filter: None,
    60	            index: None, range: None, matching: None, site: site.clone(),
    61	        });
    62	    }
    63	    pub(super) fn source_may_write(&self, body: &[S]) -> bool {
    64	        let mut body = body.to_vec();
    65	        let mut found = false;
    66	        super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body, &mut |expr| match expr {
    67	            A::Write { .. } => found = true,
    68	            // Helpers and named shapes expand later. Declining to certify
    69	            // them keeps freshness conservative without duplicating lowering's
    70	            // recursive effect analysis here.
    71	            A::Call { name, .. } if self.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) => found = true,
    72	            A::ShapeUse { .. } => found = true,
    73	            A::Shape { fields, .. } if fields.iter().any(|field| matches!(field, ast::ShapeField::NamedSpread { .. })) => found = true,
    74	            _ => {}
    75	        });
    76	        found
    77	    }
    78	    pub(super) fn point_present(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
    79	        self.point_key(read).is_some_and(|(key, roots)| roots.iter().all(|root|
    80	            self.binding(root).is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.get(&key) == Some(&Ty::Bool))))
    81	    }
    82	    pub(super) fn invalidate_points(&mut self) {
    83	        for scope in &mut self.scopes {
    84	            for binding in scope.values_mut() {
    85	                // A write invalidates these transaction facts, not the local's
    86	                // value. Clear shadowed roots too, and keep assignment revisions
    87	                // stable so a job sweep does not mistake a guard for an accumulator.
    88	                binding.fields.retain(|key, _| key.first().is_none_or(|k| {
    89	                    k != POINT && k != maintain_types::AUTHORIZATION && k != aggregate_facts::NONEMPTY
    90	                        && k != aggregate_facts::COUNT_CAPTURE
    91	                }));
    92	            }
    93	        }
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — post-write presence follows only a stable
    97	    // point key and a read grant whose deciding state the write cannot change.
    98	    pub(super) fn invalidate_after_write(&mut self, expr: &A) {
    99	        let A::Write { op: WriteOp::Update, table, fields, .. } = expr else {
   100	            self.invalidate_points(); return;
   101	        };
   102	        let Some(definition) = self.schema.table(table) else { self.invalidate_points(); return };
   103	        let mut deciding = BTreeSet::new();
   104	        definition.rules.read.fields(&mut deciding);
   105	        let independent = self.row_independent_read_proved(table);
   106	        let stable_read = independent || !fields.iter().any(|field| deciding.contains(&field.name));
   107	        // Maintained columns and self-probes can change indirectly. Lifetimes
   108	        // and through authority retain the conservative transaction barrier.
   109	        let safe = self.ordinary_write_lifetime(table) && stable_read
   110	            && definition.maintained.is_empty()
   111	            && !rule_probes(&definition.rules.read, table)
   112	            && rule_dependencies_stable(self.schema, &definition.rules.read);
   113	        for scope in &mut self.scopes {
   114	            for binding in scope.values_mut() {
   115	                binding.fields.retain(|key, _| match key.first().map(String::as_str) {
   116	                    Some(POINT) => safe && key.get(1) == Some(table) && key.get(2)
   117	                        .and_then(|index| definition.index(index)).is_some_and(|index|
   118	                            !fields.iter().any(|field| index.columns().contains(&field.name)
   119	                                || key.iter().skip(3).any(|part| part.starts_with(&format!("Some({:?}):", field.name))))),
   120	                    Some(maintain_types::AUTHORIZATION) => false,
   121	                    Some(aggregate_facts::NONEMPTY) => false,
   122	                    Some(aggregate_facts::COUNT_CAPTURE) => false,
   123	                    _ => true,
   124	                });
   125	            }
   126	        }
   127	    }
   128	
   129	    pub(super) fn ordinary_write_lifetime(&self, table: &str) -> bool {
   130	        self.schema.table(table).is_some_and(|table| table.expire.is_none()
   131	            && table.not_before.is_none() && table.through.is_none() && table.ephemeral.is_none())
   132	    }
   133	
   134	    pub(super) fn row_independent_read_proved(&self, table: &str) -> bool {
   135	        let Some(info) = self.catalog.tables.get(table) else { return false };
   136	        if self.schema.tables[table].rules.read.is_uniformly_readable() { return true; }
   137	        let Some(rule) = info.decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == ast::Effect::Read) else { return false };
   138	        fn proved(binder: &Binder<'_>, table: &str, value: &A) -> bool {
   139	            if let A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value && op == "or" {
   140	                return proved(binder, table, left) || proved(binder, table, right);
   141	            }
   142	            let mut value = value.clone();
   143	            maintain_types::normalize_rule_constants(&mut value);
   144	            let mut row_dependent = false;
   145	            super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value, &mut |expr| {
   146	                if matches!(expr, A::Focal { .. }) { row_dependent = true; }
   147	                if let A::Call { name, .. } = expr && binder.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) {
   148	                    row_dependent = true;
   149	                }
   150	                if let A::Read(read) | A::Exists { read, .. } | A::Created { read, .. } = expr {
   151	                    row_dependent |= read.table == table || binder.catalog.tables.get(&read.table)
   152	                        .is_none_or(|info| info.maintained.is_some())
   153	                        || binder.schema.table(&read.table).is_none_or(|target| !target.maintained.is_empty() || target.through.is_some());
   154	                }
   155	            });
   156	            !row_dependent && binder.point_guard_safe(&value) && binder.authorization_proved(&value, true)
   157	        }
   158	        proved(self, table, &rule.value)
   159	    }
   160	
   161	    pub(super) fn parent_rewrite(&self, value: &A) -> Option<String> {
   162	        let A::Member { base, name, .. } = value else { return None; };
   163	        let A::Read(read) = base.as_ref() else { return None; };
   164	        let info = self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table)?;
   165	        let field = Ty::from_scalar(info.columns.get(name)?);
   166	        if self.point_present(read) || field.is_optional() || info.maintained.is_some()
   167	            || info.decl.columns.iter().any(|c| c.name == *name && c.written_by.is_some() && !c.readable_by_audience) { return None; }
   168	        Some(format!("require exists {} else NOT_FOUND — a membership or required reference does not prove this row exists; alternatively bind {} ! else NOT_FOUND", print::read_expr(read), print::read_expr(read)))
   169	    }
   170	}
   171	
   172	pub(super) fn writes(body: &[Stmt]) -> bool {
   173	    ir::effects::Effects::writes_in(body)
   174	}
   175	
   176	fn rule_probes(rule: &snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr, target: &str) -> bool {
   177	    use snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr;
   178	    match rule {
   179	        RuleExpr::Exists { table, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table, .. } => table == target,
   180	        RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => items.iter().any(|rule| rule_probes(rule, target)),
   181	        RuleExpr::Not(rule) => rule_probes(rule, target),
   182	        _ => false,
   183	    }
   184	}
   185	
   186	fn rule_dependencies_stable(schema: &snapback4_core::schema::Schema, rule: &snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr) -> bool {
   187	    use snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr;
   188	    match rule {
   189	        RuleExpr::Exists { table, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table, .. } => schema.table(table)
   190	            .is_some_and(|target| target.maintained.is_empty() && target.through.is_none() && schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()),
   191	        RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => items.iter().all(|rule| rule_dependencies_stable(schema, rule)),
   192	        RuleExpr::Not(rule) => rule_dependencies_stable(schema, rule),
   193	        _ => true,
   194	    }
   195	}

   435	    /// Meet one loop edge with the zero-iteration path. A field proof survives
   436	    /// only when both paths retain the same fact; assignments additionally
   437	    /// widen the declared type and keep their revision for job carry checks.
   438	    fn join_loop_binding(&self, local: &str, old: &mut Binding, new: &Binding, site: &Site) -> Fallible<(bool, bool)> {
   439	        debug_assert_eq!(new.runtime, old.runtime);
   440	        let surviving_fields: BTreeMap<_, _> = old.fields.iter()
   441	            .filter(|(path, ty)| new.fields.get(*path) == Some(*ty))
   442	            .map(|(path, ty)| (path.clone(), ty.clone()))
   443	            .collect();
   444	        let fields_changed = surviving_fields.len() != old.fields.len();
   445	        if new.revision == old.revision {
   446	            old.fields = surviving_fields;
   447	            return Ok((fields_changed, false));
   448	        }
   449	        let declared = self.join(&old.declared, &new.declared, &joins::local(local, site), &joins::local(local, site))?;
   450	        let ty = self.join(&old.ty, &declared, &joins::local(local, site), &joins::local(local, site))?;
   451	        let type_changed = old.ty != ty || old.declared != declared;
   452	        let grows = type_changed && matches!(ty, Ty::List(..))
   453	            && new.origins.iter().any(|(_, source)| has_concat(source));
   454	        let changed = type_changed || !old.fields.is_empty();
   455	        old.fields.clear();
   456	        old.ty = ty;
   457	        old.declared = declared;
   458	        for origin in &new.origins {
   459	            if !old.origins.iter().any(|(_, source)| *source == origin.1) { old.origins.push(origin.clone()); }
   460	        }
   461	        old.revision = old.revision.max(new.revision);
   462	        Ok((changed, grows))
   463	    }
   464	
   465	    /// After `require x != null` or inside `if x != null:`, `x` is present.
   466	    pub(super) fn narrow(&mut self, condition: &A, then_branch: bool) {
   467	        // Aggregate facts may describe filtered domains, so they have a
   468	        // slightly wider write-free admission check than point facts. Record
   469	        // them once for the whole, already-evaluated condition: recursively
   470	        // recording a safe left operand would be stale when a later operand
   471	        // calls a writeful helper.
   472	        if self.aggregate_guard_safe(condition) {
   473	            self.record_aggregate_nonempty(condition, then_branch);
   474	        }
   475	        self.narrow_inner(condition, then_branch, self.point_guard_safe(condition));
   476	    }
   477	
   478	    fn narrow_inner(&mut self, condition: &A, then_branch: bool, points: bool) {
   479	        // The condition is evaluated before narrowing. If any operand may
   480	        // write, a fact observed by an earlier operand can already be stale.
   481	        if points {
   482	            self.record_maintain_authorization(condition, then_branch);
   483	        }
   484	        match condition {
   485	            A::Exists { read, .. } if then_branch && points => self.narrow_point(read),
   486	            A::Call { name, args, site } if name == super::super::intent_variants::PRESENT => self.narrow_inner(&A::Binary {
   487	                op: "!=".into(), left: Box::new(args[0].clone()), right: Box::new(A::Literal { value: Literal::Null, site: site.clone() }), site: site.clone(),
   488	            }, then_branch, points),
   489	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => self.narrow_inner(value, !then_branch, points),
   490	            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if (op == "and" && then_branch) || (op == "or" && !then_branch) => {
   491	                self.narrow_inner(left, then_branch, points);
   492	                self.narrow_inner(right, then_branch, points);
   493	            }
   494	            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "!=" || op == "=" => {
   495	                let candidate = match (left.as_ref(), right.as_ref()) {
   496	                    (A::Literal { value: Literal::Null, .. }, other) | (other, A::Literal { value: Literal::Null, .. }) => other,
   497	                    _ => return,
   498	                };
   499	                if let A::Read(read) = candidate {
   500	                    if points && (op == "!=") == then_branch { self.narrow_point(read); }
   501	                } else if let A::Name { name, .. } = candidate
   502	                    && let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(name).cloned()
   503	                {
   504	                    binding.ty = if (op == "!=") == then_branch { binding.ty.required() } else { Ty::Null };
   505	                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name.clone(), binding);
   506	                } else if let Some((name, path)) = field_path(candidate, self.focal.as_ref().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()))
   507	                    && !path.is_empty()
   508	                    && let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&name).cloned()
   509	                    && let Ok((_, ty)) = self.expr(candidate, &mut setup::Body::default())
   510	                {
   511	                    // @ref LLP 3000.007#10 — null guards refine fields of lexical snapshots, through short circuits and surviving branches.
   512	                    let present = (op == "!=") == then_branch;
   513	                    if present { binding.ty = binding.ty.required(); }
   514	                    binding.fields.insert(path, if present { ty.required() } else { Ty::Null });
   515	                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name.to_owned(), binding);
   516	                } else {
   517	                    self.narrow_call(candidate, (op == "!=") == then_branch);
   518	                }
   519	            }
   520	            _ => {}
   521	        }
   522	    }
   523	
   524	    pub(super) fn field_refinement(&self, expr: &A) -> Option<Ty> {
   525	        let (name, path) = field_path(expr, self.focal.as_ref().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()))?;
   526	        self.binding(&name)?.fields.get(&path).cloned()
   527	    }

   605	    /// Arguments belong to the caller: bind and snapshot each one before
   606	    /// introducing any parameter. `new` and reads are evaluated once, in
   607	    /// source order, even when the helper uses the parameter repeatedly.
   608	    fn helper_arguments(&mut self, name: &str, declared: &[ast::ArgDecl], args: &[A], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<BTreeMap<String, Binding>> {
   609	        if self.depth >= MAX_EXPANSION_DEPTH {
   610	            return Err(unsupported(site, "helpers nested deeper than eight levels", "flatten the helper"));
   611	        }
   612	        if args.len() != declared.len() {
   613	            return Err(type_error(site, format!("{name} takes {} arguments; received {}", declared.len(), args.len()), format!("{name}({})", declared.iter().map(|a| a.name.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))));
   614	        }
   615	        let mut bindings = BTreeMap::new();
   616	        for (declared, given) in declared.iter().zip(args) {
   617	            if bindings.contains_key(&declared.name) {
   618	                return Err(unknown(&declared.site, format!("duplicate argument {}", declared.name), "rename one helper parameter"));
   619	            }
   620	            let expected = Ty::from_scalar(&declared.type_);

   622	            // existing row parameter semantics. Only a literal needs id conversion.

   628	                }

   633	            };

   639	                }));

   642	            self.maintenance_scopes.insert(runtime.clone(), format!("helper/{name}/parameter/{}", declared.name));
   643	            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: runtime.clone(), value: expr });
   644	            bindings.insert(declared.name.clone(), Binding { runtime, ty: expected.clone(), declared: expected, parameter: true, revision: 0, fields: BTreeMap::new(), error: None, origins: Vec::new() });
   645	        }
   646	        Ok(bindings)
   647	    }
   648	
   649	    pub(super) fn fn_call(&mut self, function: &ast::FunctionDecl, args: &[A], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   650	        let bindings = self.helper_arguments(&function.name, &function.args, args, site, out)?;
   651	        let start = out.len();
   652	        let caller = std::mem::replace(&mut self.scopes, vec![bindings]);
   653	        self.declare(pure_calls::CONSTANTS, Ty::Null);
   654	        let focal = self.focal.take();
   655	        let loops = std::mem::take(&mut self.loops);
   656	        // @ref LLP 3000.019#6 — remove the measured helper/inline placement discrepancy.
   657	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.2 — placement inherits caller loops; break/continue stay lexical.
   658	        self.caller_loops += loops;
   659	        let returns = std::mem::take(&mut self.returns);
   660	        self.depth += 1;
   661	        self.call_chain.push((function.name.clone(), site.clone()));
   662	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.2 — helpers expand in the caller's placement; a return exits only this expansion.
   663	        let result = match &function.body {
   664	            ast::FunctionBody::Expression(value) => self.value_of(value, out),
   665	            ast::FunctionBody::Block(statements) => {
   666	                let mut body = setup::Body::default();
   667	                self.block(statements, &mut body, false).and_then(|()| {
   668	                    let ty = self.return_type()?;
   669	                    let result = self.temp("result");
   670	                    self.maintenance_scopes.insert(result.clone(), format!("helper/{}/result", function.name));
   671	                    let done = self.temp("returned");
   672	                    out.push(Stmt::Let { name: result.clone(), value: Expr::Literal(Value::Null) });
   673	                    out.push(Stmt::Let { name: done.clone(), value: Expr::Literal(Value::Bool(false)) });
   674	                    let ownership = setup::helper_ownership(&body);
   675	                    let code = helper_returns(body.code, &result, &done).0;
   676	                    out.extend(setup::Body { code, ownership });
   677	                    Ok((ir::var(&result), ty))
   678	                })
   679	            }
   680	        };
   681	        if let Ok((_, ty)) = &result
   682	            && let Ty::Row(table) | Ty::WriteRow(table) | Ty::Page(table, _) = ty.required()
   683	            && self.schema.receipt_dependencies.contains_key(&table) {
   684	            self.receipt_returns.push((table, function.site.clone()));
   685	        }
   686	        if let Ok((value, _)) = &result {
   687	            self.pure_functions.insert(function.name.clone(), pure_calls::statements(&out[start..]) && pure_calls::expression(value));
   688	        }
   689	        self.depth -= 1;
   690	        self.call_chain.pop();
   691	        self.returns = returns;
   692	        self.loops = loops;
   693	        self.caller_loops -= loops;
   694	        self.scopes = caller;
   695	        if point_facts::writes(&out[start..]) { self.invalidate_points(); }
   696	        self.focal = focal;
   697	        result.map_err(|diagnostic| {
   698	            if matches!(diagnostic.code.as_str(), "E_QUARRY_PLACEMENT" | "E_EMIT_IN_QUERY") || diagnostic.message == "queries cannot mint ids" {
   699	                self.errors.retain(|error| *error != diagnostic);
   700	                placement(site, format!("fn {}: {} (at {})", function.name, diagnostic.message, diagnostic.site), diagnostic.rewrite)

     1	//! One recognition of point keys versus prefix populations, shared by reads,
     2	//! loop domains, merge discovery and program existence tests.
     3	use super::*;
     4	
     5	#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
     6	pub(super) enum Position { Page, Domain, Aggregate, Exists }
     7	
     8	impl Binder<'_> {
     9	    pub(super) fn is_page(&self, read: &ReadExpr, point: ReadMode) -> Fallible<bool> {
    10	        let global = read.keys.is_empty() && self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table)
    11	            .and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref()).is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty());
    12	        if !read.order_keys.is_empty() || read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() || read.filter.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() || read.keys.is_empty() && !global { return Ok(true); }
    13	        if global { return Ok(false); }
    14	        // Speculation cannot emit setup or consume compiler names. The selected
    15	        // form binds its key expressions exactly once in the caller's body.
    16	        let mut probe = self.clone();
    17	        let info = probe.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
    18	        let mut lookup = read.clone();
    19	        if point == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.maintained.is_some() && lookup.index.is_none() { lookup.index = Some("byGroup".into()); }
    20	        match probe.select_index(info, &lookup, point, &mut setup::Body::default()) {
    21	            Ok(_) => Ok(false),
    22	            Err(error) if error.code == "E_QUARRY_AMBIGUOUS" => Err(error),
    23	            Err(point_error) => {
    24	                // Only an actual index prefix changes the form. A mistyped
    25	                // full key or an uncovered named equality keeps its point
    26	                // diagnostic, including the established repair.
    27	                let mut probe = self.clone();
    28	                match probe.select_index(info, read, ReadMode::Range, &mut setup::Body::default()) {
    29	                    Ok(_) => Ok(true),
    30	                    Err(error) if error.code == "E_QUARRY_AMBIGUOUS" => Err(error),
    31	                    Err(error) if error.code == "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN" => Err(error),
    32	                    Err(_) => Err(point_error),
    33	                }
    34	            },
    35	        }
    36	    }
    37	
    38	    pub(super) fn prefix_bound(&self, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, position: Position) -> Fallible<(ast::RangeSpec, Bound)> {
    39	        if let Some(range) = &read.range { return Ok((range.clone(), Bound::Literal(range.take))); }
    40	        let ceiling = if self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job { snapback4_core::Ceilings::job() } else { snapback4_core::Ceilings::default() }.examined;
    41	        let pinned = &index.columns[..read.keys.len()];
    42	        // One group per distinct source key: a parent-scoped source cap bounds
    43	        // its aggregate rows too, without copying a constraint onto the view.
    44	        // A pinned unique key bounds the group as a cap does (wave 3 #7); `.id = x`
    45	        // plans as the closed seek `.id >= x and .id <= x` on the next column.
    46	        let mut equal = pinned.to_vec();
    47	        if let Some(next) = index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) {
    48	            let printed = |op: &str| read.seeks.iter().filter(|seek| &seek.column == next && seek.op == op).map(|seek| crate::print::expression(&seek.value)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    49	            let (low, high) = (printed(">="), printed("<="));
    50	            if read.seeks.len() == 2 && low.len() == 1 && low == high { equal.push(next.clone()); }
    51	        }
    52	        let cap = self.schema.group_bound(&read.table, pinned).into_iter().chain(crate::bind::unique_bound::unique_bound(self.schema, &read.table, &equal)).min().or_else(|| {
    53	            let m = self.schema.maintain_for(&read.table)?;
    54	            let parent = m.parent_key.as_ref()?;
    55	            pinned.contains(&parent.reference).then(|| self.schema.group_bound(&m.source, &[parent.reference.clone()])).flatten()
    56	        });
    57	        let bound = if position == Position::Exists { Bound::Literal(1) }
    58	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.22 — ruling 24: the schema cap supplies the whole group's ordinary literal scan bound.
    59	        // Queries with a non-uniform rule keep this existing capped path;
    60	        // scan_at upgrades uniformly decided aggregates to streaming below.
    61	        else if position == Position::Aggregate && self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Query && let Some(cap) = cap.filter(|cap| u64::from(*cap) <= ceiling) { Bound::Literal(cap) }
    62	        else if position == Position::Aggregate || (position == Position::Domain && self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job) { Bound::Meter }
    63	        else if let Some(cap) = cap.filter(|cap| u64::from(*cap) <= ceiling) { Bound::Literal(cap) }
    64	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.22 — ruling 23: only query loop and lane domains may consume a prefix under the examined meter.
    65	        else if position == Position::Domain && self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Query { Bound::Meter }
    66	        else {
    67	            let columns = if pinned.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!(" by {}", pinned.join(", ")) };
    68	            let mut page = read.clone();
    69	            page.index = Some(index.name.clone());
    70	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.3 — a synced table's horizon keeps the newest rows, so its page reads newest first:
    71	            // the page rewrite and N_NO_HORIZON's window then agree, and applying both draws no N_SYNC_ORDER.
    72	            let window = self.schema.table(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.sync.as_ref()).map(|sync| sync.horizon.as_ref().map(|window| window.by.clone()));
    73	            if let Some(Some(by)) = &window
    74	                && let Some(horizon) = self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|info| info.indexes.iter().find(|candidate| &candidate.name == by))
    75	                && horizon.words.is_none() && horizon.columns.len() > pinned.len() && horizon.columns[..pinned.len()] == *pinned {
    76	                page.index = Some(horizon.name.clone());
    77	            }
    78	            let order = if window.is_some() { ast::Order::Desc } else { ast::Order::Asc };
    79	            page.range = Some(ast::RangeSpec { order, take: 50, cursor: Some(Box::new(A::Name { name: "c".into(), site: read.site.clone() })), site: read.site.clone() });
    80	            let suggestion = format!("page with `{} N` and a cursor: `{}` (declare `c: cursor ?`)", if window.is_some() { "last" } else { "first" }, self.catalog.source.expression(&A::Read(page.clone())));
    81	            // A filter pinning a whole unique key names at most one row: offer the one-row read first.
    82	            let one = (index.unique && index.words.is_none() && !pinned.is_empty() && pinned.len() == index.columns.len()).then(|| {
    83	                let mut one = page.clone();
    84	                one.range = Some(ast::RangeSpec { order: ast::Order::Asc, take: 1, cursor: None, site: read.site.clone() });
    85	                format!("`unique {}` makes this at most one row: `{}`; or ", pinned.join(", "), self.catalog.source.expression(&A::Read(one)))
    86	            }).unwrap_or_default();
    87	            let rewrite = if cap.is_none() { format!("{one}declare `cap N{columns}` on {}, or {suggestion}", read.table) } else { suggestion };
    88	            let message = match cap {
    89	                Some(cap) => format!("{}'s whole group has cap {cap}, above the {ceiling} examined ceiling; one read cannot examine the whole group", read.table),
    90	                None => format!("{} has no cap over this prefix; this position needs a bounded page", read.table),
    91	            };
    92	            return Err(if position == Position::Domain && self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Query {
    93	                placement(&read.site, format!("{message}; meter-bounded prefix loops and lane sources belong in queries"), rewrite).guide("bounds")
    94	            } else { Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_BOUND", message, rewrite).guide("bounds") });
    95	        };
    96	        if !read.seeks.is_empty() && read.order_keys.is_empty() && !matches!(position, Position::Aggregate | Position::Exists) && !(position == Position::Domain && self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job) {
    97	            return Err(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_BOUND", "a seek needs an explicit page bound", format!("{} first 50 by {}", self.catalog.source.expression(&A::Read(read.clone())), index.name)).guide("bounds"));
    98	        }
    99	        Ok((ast::RangeSpec { order: ast::Order::Asc, take: bound.maximum() as u32, cursor: None, site: read.site.clone() }, bound))
   100	    }
   101	
   102	    pub(super) fn check_meter_prefix(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, scan: &Scan, aggregate: bool) -> Fallible<()> {
   103	        if scan.take != Bound::Meter || snapback4_core::engine::rules::scan_visibility(self.schema, scan).uniform { return Ok(()); }
   104	        let table = &self.schema.tables[&scan.table];
   105	        let index = &table.indexes[&scan.index];
   106	        let pinned = index.columns().into_iter().take(scan.prefix.len()).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>();
   107	        let used = snapback4_core::engine::rules::deciding_fields(&scan.table, table, &table.rules.read);
   108	        let missing = used.difference(&pinned).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
   109	        let mut leading = missing.clone();
   110	        for column in index.columns().into_iter().skip(scan.prefix.len()) { if !leading.contains(&column) { leading.push(column); } }
   111	        let mut page = read.clone();
   112	        page.range = Some(ast::RangeSpec { order: ast::Order::Asc, take: 50, cursor: Some(Box::new(A::Name { name: "c".into(), site: read.site.clone() })), site: read.site.clone() });
   113	        // @ref LLP 3000.028#4-put-the-rules-where-the-builder-meets-them — preserve the loop, callers and truncation cost in the reason.
   114	        let jobs = self.call_chain.last().filter(|_| self.call_chain.len() > 1)
   115	            .map(|(helper, _)| self.helper_job_callers(helper)).unwrap_or_default();
   116	        let query_site = &self.call_chain[0].1;
   117	        let declaration = self.catalog.source.declarations.get(&query_site.to_string());
   118	        if aggregate && (self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Query || jobs.is_empty() || declaration.is_none()) {
   119	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#11 — a total has no first-N repair (app-farm lane 087).
   120	            let deciding = if missing.is_empty() { leading.join(", ") } else { missing.iter().map(|c| format!(".{c}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ") };
   121	            let grant = snapback4_core::engine::rules::grant_decision(self.schema, scan);
   122	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#17.8 — a readable grant the program requires before the total (`require exists staff[viewer]`)
   123	            // makes every row readable for every caller who reaches it, which the server decides once (read.rs `granted`).
   124	            let guard = grant.as_ref().map_or_else(String::new, |grant| format!("; or guard it with the grant, so every caller who reaches it reads every row: `require {} else …` before it (or `if {}:` around it)", grant.quarry(), grant.quarry()));
   125	            let diagnostic = Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_BOUND", format!("{} by {}: a total over rows the viewer may only partly read is not decided by its pinned columns, so no page bound makes it exact", scan.table, scan.index),
   126	                format!("pin {deciding} with where equalities so one verdict covers every row (the total then covers only that scope){guard}; {}. If the maintained count keeps the source's private read rule, its group keys must carry every field that rule reads (including {deciding}); a public total instead deliberately discloses the count across hidden rows; or return a `first N` page with a cursor and fold it in the client", self.total_view_recipe(read, scan))).guide("bounds");
   127	            if grant.is_some() && self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Query && self.call_chain.len() == 1 {
   128	                self.pending_totals.push((scan.site.clone(), diagnostic));
   129	                return Ok(());
   130	            }
   131	            return Err(diagnostic);
   132	        }
   133	        if self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Query || jobs.is_empty() || declaration.is_none() {
   134	            return Err(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_BOUND", format!("{} by {}: a meter-bounded prefix needs a read rule uniformly decided by its pinned columns", scan.table, scan.index),
   135	                format!("use `first N`: `{}` (declare `c: cursor ?` and follow its cursor); for a complete read, scan by an index leading with {} and pin the deciding columns (N_NEVER_COMPLETE)", self.catalog.source.expression(&A::Read(page)), leading.join(", "))).guide("bounds"));
   136	        }
   137	        let chain = self.call_chain.iter().map(|(name, site)| format!("{name} (at {site})")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" -> ");
   138	        let job = format!("the system job caller(s) {}", jobs.join(", "));
   139	        let authored = self.catalog.read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| self.catalog.source.expression(&A::Read(read.clone())));
   140	        let rule = if missing.is_empty() { "read rule".into() } else { format!("{}-based read rule", missing.join(", ")) };
   141	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#23 — a whole-query replacement points to that declaration, never its shared helper.
   142	        let end = query_site.line + declaration.unwrap().lines().count() as u32 - 1;
   143	        let context = format!("; viewer query caller chain: {chain}. `{authored}` (at {}) does not decide its {rule}; a helper grants no system authority. The replacement is for the entire unused viewer query `{}`: replace lines {}-{end} in {} with the printed comment. Keep the complete scheduled job. A separately named paged preview needs its own cursor; `first 50` is a partial preview, not an equivalent complete total. For a complete per-group read, pin {}. Inner recipient work must fit one chunk even when the outer sweep adapts its chunk size; an oversized recipient parks with E_BOUND rather than committing a partial total", read.site, self.call_chain[0].0, query_site.line, query_site.file, leading.join(", "));
   144	        Err(Diagnostic::new(query_site, "E_QUARRY_BOUND", format!("{} by {}: a meter-bounded prefix needs a read rule uniformly decided by its pinned columns{context}", scan.table, scan.index),
   145	            format!("-- {} removed: keep the complete helper under {job}'s chunk meter.", self.call_chain[0].0)).guide("bounds"))
   146	    }
   147	
   148	    /// A maintained view that keeps this total, rendered from the author's own
   149	    /// table, column and group (lanes 007 062 067: "hard to map to this schema").
   150	    // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.10 — a total over partly readable rows: the view discloses the total, never the rows.
   151	    fn total_view_recipe(&self, read: &ReadExpr, scan: &Scan) -> String {
   152	        use crate::bind::intent::{column, split};
   153	        let table = &scan.table;
   154	        let (kind, of) = self.total.clone().unwrap_or(("count", None));
   155	        // The group: the read's equalities, in its index order.
   156	        let mut groups: Vec<(String, String)> = read.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| key.column.clone().map(|column| (column, print::expression(&key.value)))).collect();
   157	        let mut filters = Vec::new();
   158	        if let Some(filter) = read.filter.clone() { split(*filter, &mut filters); }
   159	        for filter in &filters {
   160	            if let A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = filter && op == "=" && let Some(col) = column(left)
   161	                && super::super::intent::independent(right) && !groups.iter().any(|(c, _)| c == col) {
   162	                groups.push((col.to_owned(), print::expression(right)));
   163	            }
   164	        }
   165	        let suffix = if kind == "count" { "Count" } else { "Total" };
   166	        let name = format!("{table}{suffix}");
   167	        let aggregate = match &of { Some(column) => format!("sum {table} of {column}"), None => format!("count {table}") };
   168	        let (readers, who) = match snapback4_core::engine::rules::grant_decision(self.schema, scan) {
   169	            Some(grant) => (grant.quarry(), "the callers that grant admits"),
   170	            None => ("allow".to_owned(), "everyone; narrow it to the readers the total is for"),
   171	        };
   172	        if groups.is_empty() {
   173	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#17.2 — a view needs a group key (the core refuses an empty `by`): a whole-table
   174	            // total groups by a column every row holds the same value of (lane 020 pasted `count t` and got E_QUARRY_SYNTAX).
   175	            let key = (1..).map(|n| if n == 1 { "totalKey".to_owned() } else { format!("totalKey{n}") })
   176	                .find(|key| !table_columns(self.schema, table).contains(key)).unwrap();
   177	            return format!("or keep the total in a view, which discloses the total, never the rows; a view groups by a key, so give every {table} row the same one: add `{key}: enum('all') default 'all'` and `immutable {key}` to {table}, declare `view {name}: {aggregate} by .{key}` with `read <- {readers}` ({who}), then read `{name}['all']`");
   178	        }
   179	        let by = format!(" by {}", groups.iter().map(|(c, _)| format!(".{c}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));
   180	        let lookup = format!("{name}[{}]", groups.iter().map(|(_, v)| v.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));
   181	        format!("or keep the total in a view, which discloses the total, never the rows: `view {name}: {aggregate}{by}` with `read <- {readers}` ({who}), then read `{lookup}`")
   182	    }
   183	
   184	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.22 — ruling 25: program existence over a prefix is a top-1 scan below page ownership; rule probes retain their exact-key path.
   185	    pub(super) fn exists_read(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   186	        if self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref())
   187	            .is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty())
   188	        {
   189	            return Err(type_error(&read.site, format!("{} is one global maintained scalar, not an existence probe", read.table),
   190	                format!("compare the bare scalar instead: `{} > 0`", read.table)).guide("maintains"));
   191	        }
   192	        self.exists_probe(read, out).map_err(|error| super::super::bracket_ranges::refusal(read, false, error))
   193	    }
   194	
   195	    fn exists_probe(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   196	        if !self.is_page(read, ReadMode::Exists)? { return Ok((self.read_value(read, ReadMode::Exists, out)?, Ty::Bool)); }
   197	        let (scan, _) = self.scan_at(read, out, Position::Exists)?;
   198	        Ok((Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Gt, left: Box::new(Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Count, args: vec![Expr::Scan(scan)] }), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Int(0))) }, Ty::Bool))
   199	    }
   200	}
   201	
   202	fn table_columns(schema: &snapback4_core::schema::Schema, table: &str) -> Vec<String> {
   203	    schema.table(table).map(|t| t.columns.keys().cloned().collect()).unwrap_or_default()
   204	}
     1	//! Resolve row extrema without changing the binary min/max overload.
     2	use super::*;
     3	
     4	impl Binder<'_> {
     5	    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.2 — literal columns retain their encoding and seek;
     6	    // other scalar values bind to the aggregate row and fold the whole scan.
     7	    pub(super) fn extremum(&mut self, name: &str, args: &[A], out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<(Expr, Ty)>> {
     8	        if matches!(&args[1], A::Member { base, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. })) {
     9	            return self.column_extremum(name, args, out);
    10	        }
    11	        let candidate = self.aggregate_domain(&args[0]).or_else(|| {
    12	            self.catalog.extremum_domains.get(&args[0].site().to_string()).filter(|read|
    13	                !self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref())
    14	                    .is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty())).cloned()
    15	        });
    16	        let domain = if let Some(read) = candidate {
    17	            self.is_page(&read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint)?.then_some(read)
    18	        } else { None };
    19	        let nonempty = domain.as_ref().is_some_and(|read| self.aggregate_nonempty(read));
    20	        let mut probe = self.clone();
    21	        let source_ty = match probe.expr(&args[0], &mut setup::Body::default()) {
    22	            Ok((_, ty)) => {
    23	                if ty.sequence().is_none() { return Ok(None); }
    24	                Some(ty)
    25	            }
    26	            Err(error) if domain.is_none() => return Err(error),
    27	            Err(_) => None,
    28	        };
    29	        let outer = probe.expr(&args[1], &mut setup::Body::default());
    30	        if let Ok((_, other)) = &outer {
    31	            if !other.is_optional() && source_ty.as_ref().is_some_and(|ty| ty.comparable(other)) {
    32	                return Ok(None); // the existing binary collection overload
    33	            }
    34	            // An expression which needs the enclosing focal row cannot silently
    35	            // switch to the aggregate row. Removing just that focal binding also
    36	            // distinguishes nested shapes/aggregates that own their own `.`.
    37	            probe.focal = None;
    38	            if self.focal.is_some() && probe.expr(&args[1], &mut setup::Body::default()).is_err() {
    39	                return Err(ambiguous(args[1].site(), format!("{name}'s value expression can bind the enclosing row"),
    40	                    format!("move `{name}({}, {})` into a helper outside the enclosing shape; pass enclosing values as named arguments", print::expression(&args[0]), print::expression(&args[1]))).guide("reads"));
    41	            }
    42	        }
    43	        let (source, item, domain) = if let Some(read) = domain {
    44	            let (scan, _) = self.scan_at(&read, out, prefix::Position::Aggregate)?;
    45	            self.shape_meter_note(&scan, &read);
    46	            (Expr::Scan(scan), Ty::Row(read.table), true)
    47	        } else {
    48	            let (source, ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
    49	            (source, ty.sequence().expect("probed sequence").0, false)
    50	        };
    51	        let saved = self.focal.replace(("$filter".into(), item.clone()));
    52	        let predicate = std::mem::replace(&mut self.in_predicate, false);
    53	        let kind = std::mem::replace(&mut self.kind, ProgramKind::Query);
    54	        self.push_scope();
    55	        self.declare("$filter", item);
    56	        let mut body = setup::Body::default();
    57	        let projected = self.expr(&args[1], &mut body);
    58	        self.pop_scope();
    59	        self.kind = kind;
    60	        self.in_predicate = predicate;
    61	        self.focal = saved;
    62	        let (value, ty) = projected?;
    63	        if matches!(ty.required(), Ty::List(..) | Ty::Collection(..) | Ty::Page(..) | Ty::Object(..) | Ty::RowShape(..) | Ty::RowObject(..) | Ty::Row(..) | Ty::WriteRow(..)) {
    64	            return Err(type_error(args[1].site(), format!("{name} needs a scalar row expression, got {}", ty.name()),
    65	                format!("{name}({}, .column + 1)", print::expression(&args[0]))));
    66	        }
    67	        let value = if body.is_empty() { value } else { Expr::Block { body: body.code, value: Box::new(value) } };
    68	        let result = if nonempty && !ty.is_optional() { ty } else { ty.optional() };
    69	        Ok(Some((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::ExtremumOf { max: name == "max", domain }, args: vec![source, value] }, result)))
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    // @ref LLP 3000.013#5.1 — domains retain aggregate admission and coverage.
    73	    fn column_extremum(&mut self, name: &str, args: &[A], out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<(Expr, Ty)>> {
    74	        let A::Member { base, name: field, .. } = &args[1] else { return Ok(None); };
    75	        if !matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. }) { return Ok(None); }
    76	        // Speculation must not emit setup or consume fresh names for a scalar
    77	        // binary call such as min(value, .column) inside an existing shape.
    78	        let mut probe = self.clone();
    79	        let domain = if let Some(read) = self.aggregate_domain(&args[0]) {
    80	            self.is_page(&read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint)?.then_some(read)
    81	        } else { None };
    82	        let probed = match probe.expr(&args[0], &mut setup::Body::default()) {
    83	            Ok(value) => Some(value),
    84	            Err(error) if domain.is_none() => return Err(error),
    85	            // A streaming domain need not be a valid materialized page.
    86	            Err(_) => None,
    87	        };
    88	        if let Some((_, ty)) = probed {
    89	            let Some((item, _)) = ty.sequence() else { return Ok(None); };
    90	            if let Ok((_, other)) = probe.expr(&args[1], &mut setup::Body::default())
    91	                && !other.is_optional() && ty.comparable(&other) {
    92	                if self.member(Expr::Literal(Value::Null), item, field, args[1].site()).is_ok() {
    93	                    return Err(ambiguous(args[1].site(), format!("{name} can compare two collections or aggregate .{field} from the first"),
    94	                        format!("bind `other = .{field}` before this call and write `{name}({}, other)` for the binary comparison; for an aggregate, call `{name}(rows, .{field})` outside the enclosing shape", print::expression(&args[0]))).guide("reads"));
    95	                }
    96	                return Ok(None);
    97	            }
    98	        }
    99	        let nonempty = domain.as_ref().is_some_and(|read| self.aggregate_nonempty(read));
   100	        let (source, item, domain) = if let Some(mut read) = domain {
   101	            // Bare table names reach the binder without intent planning.
   102	            if matches!(&args[0], A::Name { .. }) {
   103	                let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
   104	                if let Some(index) = info.indexes.iter().filter(|index| index.is_plain() && index.columns.first() == Some(field)).min_by_key(|index| &index.name) {
   105	                    read.index = Some(index.name.clone());
   106	                }
   107	            }
   108	            let (mut scan, _) = self.scan_at(&read, out, prefix::Position::Aggregate)?;
   109	            if scan.orders_field(self.schema, field) {
   110	                scan.order = if name == "max" { Order::Desc } else { Order::Asc };
   111	                // Nulls contribute no extremum. Seek past their entire index
   112	                // segment, including when there are no present values.
   113	                if self.schema.tables[&read.table].columns.get(field).is_some_and(|column| matches!(column, snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Optional(_))) {
   114	                    let range = scan.range.get_or_insert(ir::Range { gt: None, gte: None, lt: None, lte: None });
   115	                    if range.gt.is_none() && range.gte.is_none() { range.gt = Some(Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))); }
   116	                }
   117	            }
   118	            // The runtime seeks the first qualifying ordered value. Price that
   119	            // candidate limit for advice only; the aggregate IR keeps its domain.
   120	            // Expired rows are charged and skipped before a value qualifies.
   121	            let mut advice = scan.clone();
   122	            if scan.orders_field(self.schema, field) && !self.schema.tables[&read.table].is_expiring() { advice.take = Bound::Literal(1); }
   123	            self.shape_meter_note(&advice, &read);
   124	            (Expr::Scan(scan), Ty::Row(read.table), true)
   125	        } else {
   126	            let (source, ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   127	            (source, ty.sequence().expect("probed sequence").0, false)
   128	        };
   129	        let (_, ty) = self.member(Expr::Literal(Value::Null), item, field, args[1].site())?;
   130	        // Column ordering uses the same total order as binary min/max. Nulls
   131	        // contribute no value, including when every row's column is null.
   132	        let result = if nonempty && !ty.is_optional() { ty } else { ty.optional() };
   133	        Ok(Some((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Extremum { field: field.clone(), max: name == "max", domain }, args: vec![source] }, result)))
   134	    }
   135	}

    82	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.3 — a seek constrains the first index column after the pinned prefix.
    83	    pub(super) fn seek(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<ir::Range>> {
    84	        if read.seeks.is_empty() { return Ok(None); }
    85	        let mut range = ir::Range { gt: None, gte: None, lt: None, lte: None };
    86	        for bound in &read.seeks {
    87	            if index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) != Some(&bound.column) || read.matching.is_some() {
    88	                return Err(type_error(&bound.site, "a seek compares the first unpinned column of a plain index", "t[prefix, col >= start, col < end] first 50 by index"));
    89	            }
    90	            let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, &bound.column);
    91	            let (value, ty) = self.field_value(&ObjectField { spread: false, name: bound.column.clone(), value: Some(bound.value.clone()), site: bound.site.clone() }, &expected, out)?;
    92	            if !ty.comparable(&expected) || ty.is_optional() {
    93	                let endpoint = print::expression(&bound.value);
    94	                let rewrite = if ty.is_optional() {
    95	                    format!("put the read inside `if {endpoint} != null:`, then `{} where .{} {} {endpoint}`", read.table, bound.column, bound.op)
    96	                } else {
    97	                    format!("use a present {} endpoint in `{} where .{} {} {endpoint}`", expected.required().name(), read.table, bound.column, bound.op)
    98	                };
    99	                return Err(type_error(&bound.site, format!("seek on {}.{} expects present {}, got {}", read.table, bound.column, expected.required().name(), ty.name()), rewrite));
   100	            }
   101	            let slot = match bound.op.as_str() { ">" => &mut range.gt, ">=" => &mut range.gte, "<" => &mut range.lt, "<=" => &mut range.lte, _ => unreachable!("parser seek comparison") };
   102	            if slot.is_some() { return Err(type_error(&bound.site, "a seek bound is repeated", "write one lower and one upper bound")); }
   103	            *slot = Some(Box::new(self.snapshot(value, out)));
   104	        }
   105	        if (range.gt.is_some() && range.gte.is_some()) || (range.lt.is_some() && range.lte.is_some()) {
   106	            return Err(type_error(&read.site, "a seek has at most one lower and one upper bound", "t[prefix, col >= start, col < end] first 50 by index"));
   107	        }
   108	        // Null sorts below every present value. An upper-only nullable seek
   109	        // must skip the entire null segment, including its id suffixes.
   110	        if range.gt.is_none() && range.gte.is_none()
   111	            && read.seeks.first().is_some_and(|bound| self.key_ty(info, index, &bound.column).is_optional()) {
   112	            range.gt = Some(Box::new(ir::lit(Value::Null)));
   113	        }
   114	        Ok(Some(range))
   115	    }

   119	    pub(super) fn variant(&self) -> Option<ir::VariantId> {

   126	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.5 — a declared page shape after a scalar predicate needs no wrapper or correction loop.
   127	    pub(super) fn page_shape(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<A> {

   144	    pub(super) fn scan_filter(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl) -> Fallible<(ReadExpr, Option<Box<Expr>>)> {
   145	        let mut read = read.clone();

   156	            let mut residual = Vec::new();

   160	                        .filter(|(column, _)| !self.key_ty(info, index, column).is_optional())
   161	                        .map(|(column, value)| (column, vec![">=".into(), "<=".into()], value)));

   164	                    && super::super::intent_aggregates::safe_endpoint(&value, &required)

   166	                    for op in ops { read.seeks.push(ast::SeekBound { column: column.clone(), op, value: value.clone(), site: part.site().clone() }); }
   167	                } else { residual.push(part); }
   168	            }
   169	            let Some(remaining) = super::super::intent::conjunction(residual) else { return Ok((read, None)); };
   170	            predicate = remaining;
   171	        }
   172	        // A single next-column comparison is the same seek as its key spelling.
   173	        if read.seeks.is_empty() && read.matching.is_none()
   174	            && let A::Binary { op, left, right, site } = predicate.as_ref()

   203	                format!("{} by {}: this unscoped `where` pins no index prefix; {consequence}", read.table, index.name),

   209	
   210	    pub(super) fn row_predicate(&mut self, table: &str, predicate: &A) -> Fallible<Expr> {
   211	        let row_ty = Ty::Row(table.into());
   212	        let prior = self.focal.replace(("$filter".into(), row_ty.clone()));
   213	        let kind = std::mem::replace(&mut self.kind, ProgramKind::Query);
   214	        let predicate_context = std::mem::replace(&mut self.in_predicate, true);
   215	        let mut body = setup::Body::default();
   216	        self.push_scope();
   217	        // A fresh lexical row snapshot lets short-circuit guards narrow its
   218	        // fields without leaking facts to another scan or an enclosing row.
   219	        self.scopes.last_mut().unwrap().insert("$filter".into(), Binding {
   220	            runtime: "$filter".into(), declared: row_ty.clone(), ty: row_ty,
   221	            parameter: false, revision: 0, fields: BTreeMap::new(), error: None, origins: Vec::new(),
   222	        });
   223	        let bound = self.expr(predicate, &mut body);
   224	        self.pop_scope();
   225	        self.kind = kind;
   226	        self.in_predicate = predicate_context;
   227	        self.focal = prior;
   228	        let (value, ty) = bound?;
   229	        if !ty.is_bool() { return Err(type_error(predicate.site(), format!("where needs a bool predicate, got {}", ty.name()), "where .col = value")); }
   230	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.6 — predicate setup, including lazy reads, runs afresh for each rule-visible row.
   231	        Ok(if body.is_empty() { value } else { Expr::Block { body: body.code, value: Box::new(value) } })
   232	    }
   233	}
   234	
   235	fn pins_leading_column(expr: &A, column: Option<&str>) -> bool {
   236	    let focal_column = |value: &A| matches!(value, A::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. }) && Some(name.as_str()) == column);
   237	    match expr {
   238	        A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => pins_leading_column(left, column) || pins_leading_column(right, column),
   239	        A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => pins_leading_column(left, column) && pins_leading_column(right, column),
   240	        A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" => (focal_column(left) && !has_focal(right)) || (focal_column(right) && !has_focal(left)),
   241	        _ => false,
   242	    }
   243	}
   244	
   245	fn has_focal(expr: &A) -> bool {
   246	    match expr {
   247	        A::Focal { .. } => true,
   248	        A::Name { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => false,
   249	        A::Member { base, .. } | A::Unary { value: base, .. } => has_focal(base),
   250	        A::Binary { left, right, .. } => has_focal(left) || has_focal(right),
   251	        A::Call { args, .. } => args.iter().any(has_focal),
   252	        // Other forms stay predicates; a read on a row-dependent key must not escape the row.
   253	        _ => true,
   254	    }
   255	}

   433	fn bind_target_arm(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, sync: &SyncDecl, table: &str, prefix: &[String], principal: Option<&str>) -> Fallible<Audience> {
   434	    let Some(target) = catalog.tables.get(table) else {

   439	            return Err(sync_error(&sync.site, format!("sync to {table}[.{column}] names an undeclared column of {}", info.decl.name), name_rewrite(column, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str), None)));
   440	        }
   441	    }
   442	    let principals: Vec<&str> = target.columns.iter().filter(|(_, kind)| matches!(kind.kind, TypeKind::Principal) && !kind.optional).map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect();
   443	    let principal = match principal {
   444	        Some(named) => {
   445	            if !principals.contains(&named) {
   446	                return Err(sync_error(&sync.site, format!("sync to {table}[..] by .{named}: {table}.{named} is not a principal column"), format!("sync to {table}[{}] by .<principal column of {table}>", prefix.iter().map(|c| format!(".{c}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))));

   459	/// The arms a read rule inverts to: one audience, or a union of
   460	/// principal-column arms and at most one group arm (LLP 3000.036.000.000 §2.2).
   461	fn infer_arms(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, sync: &SyncDecl, rule: &RuleExpr, source: &str, named: Option<&str>) -> Fallible<Vec<Audience>> {
   462	    let audience = super::intent_sync::audience_rule(&info.decl.name, info, rule);
   463	    let disjuncts = audience.disjuncts();
   464	    if disjuncts.len() < 2 || disjuncts.iter().any(|disjunct| matches!(disjunct, RuleExpr::Public { .. } | RuleExpr::Allow)) {
   465	        return infer_audience(catalog, info, sync, &audience, rule, source, named).map(|audience| vec![audience]);
   466	    }
   467	    let mut arms = Vec::new();
   468	    for disjunct in &disjuncts {
   469	        let arm = match disjunct {
   470	            RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(column), RuleTerm::Viewer) | RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer, RuleTerm::Field(column)) => Audience::Column(column.clone()),
   471	            RuleExpr::Exists { .. } => infer_audience(catalog, info, sync, disjunct, rule, source, named)?,
   472	            _ => return Err(union_refusal(catalog, info, sync, rule, source)),
   473	        };
   474	        if !arms.contains(&arm) { arms.push(arm); }
   475	    }
   476	    let groups: Vec<String> = arms.iter().filter_map(|arm| match arm { Audience::Target { .. } => Some(arm.arm()), _ => None }).collect();
   477	    if groups.len() > 1 {
   478	        return Err(sync_error(&sync.site, format!("a union syncs to at most one group audience; `{source}` has {} ({})", groups.len(), groups.join(", ")),
   479	            format!("give the second group's readers rows in {}'s membership table, so one group admits them all; or replace the sync line with `online only` and keep this rule (guide sharing)", groups[0])).guide("sharing"));
   480	    }
   481	    if named.is_some() && groups.is_empty() {
   482	        return Err(sync_error(&sync.site, format!("`sync by .{}` names a group audience's recipient column; `{source}` has no group audience", named.unwrap_or_default()), "sync"));
   483	    }
   484	    if arms.len() == 1 { return Ok(arms); }
   485	    Ok(canonical(arms))
   486	}

   488	fn union_refusal(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, sync: &SyncDecl, rule: &RuleExpr, source: &str) -> Diagnostic {
   489	    sync_error(&sync.site, format!("sync cannot infer an audience from `{source}`: a synced table's read rule must be `public`, `.column = viewer`, `exists target[.columns, viewer]`, or an `or` of whole `.column = viewer` disjuncts and at most one `exists target[.columns, viewer]`, so each device holds every row its viewer may read and no other; do not make private rows public to get past this"), audience_split(catalog, info, rule))
   490	}
   491	
   492	// @ref LLP 3000.007#6.6 — omitted audiences invert exactly the same three read rules.
   493	fn infer_audience(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, sync: &SyncDecl, audience: &RuleExpr, rule: &RuleExpr, source: &str, named: Option<&str>) -> Fallible<Audience> {
   494	    let refusal = || union_refusal(catalog, info, sync, rule, source);
   495	    match audience {
   496	        RuleExpr::Public { .. } | RuleExpr::Allow if named.is_none() => Ok(Audience::Public),
   497	        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(column), RuleTerm::Viewer) | RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer, RuleTerm::Field(column)) if named.is_none() => Ok(Audience::Column(column.clone())),
   498	        RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } if equalities.is_empty() && key.last() == Some(&RuleTerm::Viewer) => {
   499	            let prefix = key[..key.len() - 1].iter().map(|term| match term { RuleTerm::Field(column) => Some(column.clone()), _ => None }).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>().ok_or_else(refusal)?;
   500	            let target = catalog.tables.get(table).ok_or_else(refusal)?;
   501	            let probe = target.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == index).ok_or_else(refusal)?;
   502	            let recipient = probe.columns.get(key.len() - 1).ok_or_else(refusal)?;
   503	            let principals: Vec<_> = target.columns.iter().filter(|(_, ty)| ty.kind == TypeKind::Principal && !ty.optional).map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect();
   504	            if principals.len() > 1 && named.is_none() {
   505	                return Err(sync_error(&sync.site, format!("sync: {table} has several principal columns ({}); name the recipient with `by .{recipient}`", principals.join(", ")), format!("sync by .{recipient}{}", sync.horizon.as_ref().map(|(n, by)| format!(" last {n} by {by}")).unwrap_or_default())));
   506	            }
   507	            if named.is_some_and(|name| name != recipient) || !principals.contains(&recipient.as_str()) { return Err(sync_error(&sync.site, format!("the read rule binds viewer to {table}.{recipient}"), format!("sync by .{recipient}"))); }
   508	            Ok(Audience::Target { table: table.clone(), prefix, principal: recipient.clone() })
   509	        }
   510	        _ => Err(refusal()),
   511	    }
   512	}
   513	
   514	/// The paved splits that keep every reader (LLP 3000.036 §1 A), spelled
   515	/// against this table's own columns. A union of whole audiences syncs
   516	/// (LLP 3000.036.000.000), so what reaches here is a branch that is not one
   517	/// audience: a shared (viewer-free) branch, or a branch with an extra
   518	/// condition or a literal key.
   519	// @ref LLP 3000.036.000.000#2.2 — the refusal names the branch that is not a whole audience.
   520	fn audience_split(_catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, rule: &RuleExpr) -> String {
   521	    fn viewer(rule: &RuleExpr) -> bool {
   522	        let term = |term: &RuleTerm| matches!(term, RuleTerm::Viewer);
   523	        match rule {
   524	            RuleExpr::Public { .. } | RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Deny => false,
   525	            RuleExpr::Eq(l, r) | RuleExpr::Ne(l, r) | RuleExpr::Compare(_, l, r) => term(l) || term(r),
   526	            RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => items.iter().any(viewer),
   527	            RuleExpr::Not(inner) => viewer(inner),
   528	            RuleExpr::Exists { key, equalities, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { key, equalities, .. } => key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_, t)| t)).any(term),
   529	        }
   530	    }
   531	    let whole = |rule: &RuleExpr| match rule {
   532	        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(_), RuleTerm::Viewer) | RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer, RuleTerm::Field(_)) => true,
   533	        RuleExpr::Exists { key, equalities, .. } => equalities.is_empty() && key.last() == Some(&RuleTerm::Viewer)
   534	            && key[..key.len() - 1].iter().all(|term| matches!(term, RuleTerm::Field(_))),
   535	        _ => false,
   730	                if let (Ty::Principal, other) | (other, Ty::Principal) = (&lt, &rt)
   731	                    && !matches!(other, Ty::Principal | Ty::Id)
   732	                    && !matches!((&l, &r), (RuleTerm::Viewer, _) | (_, RuleTerm::Viewer))
   733	                {
   734	                    return Err(type_error(site,
   735	                        format!("rule comparison joins {} with {}; a principal value cannot equal a value that may be null", lt.name(), rt.name()),
   736	                        "compare required principal columns, or copy the present principal into a required immutable field when the mutation creates this row"));
   737	                }
   738	                if ordered && !transition {
   739	                    // An insert rule orders only the new row's own required
   740	                    // int/time/decimal/money columns and integer literals (a
   741	                    // literal beside decimal or money is minor units, as the
   742	                    // wire and programs read it).
   743	                    // @ref LLP 3000.036#12 — money and decimal hold integer minor units, so they order like int.
   744	                    let own = |term: &RuleTerm| match term { RuleTerm::Field(field) => info.columns.contains_key(field), RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Int(_)) => true, _ => false };
   745	                    if !own(&l) || !own(&r) || matches!((&l, &r), (RuleTerm::Literal(_), RuleTerm::Literal(_))) {

   815	fn rule_term(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, expr: &Expr, bindings: &RuleBindings) -> Fallible<(RuleTerm, Ty)> {
   816	    let site = expr.site();
   817	    match expr {
   818	        Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer" => Ok((RuleTerm::Viewer, Ty::Principal)),
   819	        Expr::Member { base, name, .. } => match base.as_ref() {
   820	            Expr::Read(read) if read.keys.len() == 1 && read.keys[0].column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c| c == "id")
   821	                && read.range.is_none() && read.filter.is_none() && read.matching.is_none()
   822	                && read.index.is_none() && read.seeks.is_empty() => {
   823	                let result = rule_reference(catalog, info, &read.keys[0].value, name, bindings, site)?;
   824	                if result.0.references().last().is_some_and(|(table, _)| table == read.table) { Ok(result) }
   825	                else { Err(type_error(site, "the lookup table differs from the reference type", "use the reference's declared table")) }
   826	            }
   827	            Expr::Focal { .. } => {
   828	                if bindings.update && bindings.old.is_some() {
   829	                    return Err(unknown(site, "an update (old, next) rule names its images; . is not bound", format!("next.{name}")));
   830	                }
   831	                let ty = column_ty(info, name).ok_or_else(|| unknown(site, format!("rule reads undeclared column {}.{name}", info.decl.name), name_rewrite(name, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)))?;
   832	                Ok((RuleTerm::Field(name.clone()), ty))
   833	            }
   834	            Expr::Name { name: binder, .. } if bindings.old.as_deref() == Some(binder) => {
   835	                let ty = column_ty(info, name).ok_or_else(|| unknown(site, format!("rule reads undeclared column {}.{name}", info.decl.name), name_rewrite(name, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)))?;
   836	                Ok((RuleTerm::OldField(name.clone()), ty))
   837	            }
   838	            Expr::Name { name: binder, .. } if bindings.next.as_deref() == Some(binder) => {
   839	                let ty = column_ty(info, name).ok_or_else(|| unknown(site, format!("rule reads undeclared column {}.{name}", info.decl.name), name_rewrite(name, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)))?;
   840	                Ok((RuleTerm::NextField(name.clone()), ty))
   841	            }
   842	            Expr::Name { name: binder, .. } if bindings.update && bindings.old.is_none()
   843	                && matches!(binder.as_str(), "old" | "next") => Err(Diagnostic::new(site,
   844	                    "E_QUARRY_RULE_OPERATOR",
   845	                    format!("`{binder}.{name}` names a row image, but this update rule did not bind old and next"),
   846	                    "write this as a named transition").guide("rules")),
   847	            other => rule_reference(catalog, info, other, name, bindings, site),
   848	        },
   849	        Expr::Name { name, .. } if bindings.update && (bindings.old.as_deref() == Some(name) || bindings.next.as_deref() == Some(name)) => Err(unknown(site, format!("select a field of {name} explicitly"), format!("{name}.id"))),
   850	        Expr::Name { name, .. } => Err(unknown(site, format!("`{name}` is not a rule term; rules read the row as .{name}"), format!(".{name}"))),
   851	        Expr::Literal { value, .. } => Ok(match value {
   852	            Literal::Str(text) => (RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(text.clone())), Ty::Text(text.chars().count() as u32)),
   853	            Literal::Number(text) => (RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Int(super::integer_literal(text, site)?)), Ty::Int),
   854	            Literal::Bool(b) => (RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Bool(*b)), Ty::Bool),
   855	            Literal::Null => (RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null), Ty::Null),
   856	        }),
   857	        Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "-" => {
   858	            let (term, _) = rule_term(catalog, info, value, bindings)?;
   859	            match term {
   860	                RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Int(n)) => Ok((RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Int(-n)), Ty::Int)),
   861	                _ => Err(type_error(site, "a negative rule literal needs an integer", "compare .column with an integer literal")),
   862	            }
   863	        }
   864	        other => {
   865	            let _ = catalog;
   866	            Err(unknown(site, format!("`{}` is not a rule term", crate::print::expression(other)), "compare .column with viewer or a literal"))
   867	        }
   868	    }
   869	}
   870	
   871	fn column_ty(info: &TableInfo, name: &str) -> Option<Ty> {
   872	    if name == "id" {
   873	        return Some(Ty::Ref(info.decl.name.clone()));
   874	    }
   875	    info.columns.get(name).or_else(|| info.captures.get(name)).map(Ty::from_scalar)
   876	}

   878	/// Final-insert obligations retain their exact-index write-path contract.
   879	/// Ordinary effect rules use `probe` with residual equalities enabled.
   880	pub fn bind_probe(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, site: &Site, update: bool) -> Fallible<(String, String, Vec<RuleTerm>)> {
   881	    let bindings = RuleBindings { old: None, next: None, update, insert: false, hops: true, present: Vec::new() };
   882	    let (table, index, key, _) = probe(catalog, info, read, site, &bindings, false).map_err(|mut error| {
   883	        if error.code == "E_QUARRY_RULE_HOP" { error.rewrite = reference::reference_rewrite(catalog, info, &Expr::Exists { read: read.clone(), site: site.clone() }); }
   884	        error
   885	    })?;
   886	    Ok((table, index, key))
   887	}

    17	fn target_group_key(maintain: &Maintain, key: &[Value]) -> Vec<Value> {

    21	#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    22	fn has_legacy_maintained_reference(
    23	    kind: &crate::schema::ColumnType,
    24	    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
    25	) -> bool {
    26	    match kind {
    27	        crate::schema::ColumnType::Ref { table } => ids.get(table).is_some_and(|ids| !ids.is_empty()),
    28	        crate::schema::ColumnType::Optional(inner)
    29	        | crate::schema::ColumnType::List { of: inner, .. }
    30	        | crate::schema::ColumnType::ListRange { of: inner, .. } => has_legacy_maintained_reference(inner, ids),
    31	        _ => false,
    32	    }
    33	}
    34	
    35	#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    36	fn rewrite_legacy_maintained_reference(
    37	    kind: &crate::schema::ColumnType,
    38	    value: &mut Value,
    39	    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
    40	) -> bool {
    41	    match (kind, value) {
    42	        (crate::schema::ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => {
    43	            let Some(next) = ids.get(table).and_then(|ids| ids.get(id)) else { return false };
    44	            *id = next.clone();
    45	            true
    46	        }
    47	        (crate::schema::ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) => rewrite_legacy_maintained_reference(inner, value, ids),
    48	        (crate::schema::ColumnType::List { of, .. } | crate::schema::ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(values)) =>
    49	            values.iter_mut().fold(false, |changed, value| rewrite_legacy_maintained_reference(of, value, ids) || changed),
    50	        _ => false,
    51	    }
    52	}
